### [18] [Secret MCP: Evidence-Bounded and Context-Isolated Design Specification Generation from Web Screenshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24944)
*Yeongjin Jo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Secret MCP，一个开源本地系统，用于从公共网页参考生成可审计的设计规范文档。系统通过分离检索、证据准备、模型调用、存储和检查各阶段，采用顺序处理和多段式19节合约来保证每个引用生成独立、可追溯的设计规格，并在实验中发现零跨引用标识符污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Screenshot-to-code系统仅针对渲染实现进行优化，但截图遗漏了文档结构、交互逻辑、响应式规则以及区分观测与猜测所需的数据来源信息。此外，多参考提示存在一个引用的证据或推断污染另一个引用的风险。因此需要一种能够产生可审计、单一来源设计规范的系统。

Method: Secret MCP采用模块化架构，将检索、证据准备、模型调用、存储和检查进行分离。长截图被调整尺寸并以重叠方式平铺；证据记录保留prepared-space和source-space坐标及测量色彩调色板。采用19节合约覆盖页面清单、导航几何、响应式矩阵、组件、可访问性、验收标准等，并对measure/observe/infer/unknown等声明进行明确标注。引用通过采样器接口顺序处理，MCP适配器每次请求使用includeContext=none。

Result: 在c130c9c提交上进行两级评估：实时检索与模型集成测试中，选择两个引用（排除第三个），准备9个证据图像，发出2次采样请求，产生2个文档且零跨引用标识符出现。静态审计3个外部生成的设计索引，发现所有文档均包含19个必需章节，每个文档有唯一引用标识符，8个页面规格，1,943个像素值测量和319个颜色字面量。

Conclusion: 这些测试确立了编排不变性和语法契约合规性，而非语义或视觉重建准确性。采样器抽象在直接模型API和传输中保持了这些边界，尽管MCP采样在2026年面临弃用。

Abstract: Screenshot-to-code systems optimize for rendered implementations, but screenshots omit document structure, interaction logic, responsive rules, and provenance needed to distinguish observation from guesswork. Multi-reference prompts also risk contaminating one reference with evidence or inferences from another. We present Secret MCP, an open-source local system that produces one auditable design specification per public web reference. It separates retrieval, evidence preparation, model invocation, storage, and inspection. Long captures are resized and tiled with overlap; evidence records preserve prepared- and source-space coordinates and a measured color palette. A 19-section contract covers page inventories, navigation geometry, responsive matrices, components, accessibility, acceptance criteria, and explicit labels for measured, observed, inferred, and unknown claims. References are processed sequentially through a sampler interface. The evaluated MCP adapter sends one sampling/createMessage request per reference with includeContext set to none; a fresh-process adapter provides a stronger boundary.
  We evaluate commit c130c9c at two levels. A live retrieval and fixture-model integration run selected two references after excluding a third, prepared nine evidence images, issued two sampling requests, and produced two documents with zero cross-reference identifier occurrences. A static audit of three externally generated design indexes found all 19 required sections in every document, one unique reference identifier per document, eight page specifications, 1,943 pixel-valued measurements, and 319 color literals. These tests establish orchestration invariants and syntactic contract compliance, not semantic or visual reconstruction accuracy. The sampler abstraction preserves these boundaries across direct model APIs and transports despite MCP sampling's 2026 deprecation.

</details>


### [19] [The Evolution of Binary Decompilation in the Modern Era: A Taxonomy, Literature Review, and Future Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24955)
*Omar Abusabha,Sungjae Hwang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对过去数十年的反编译研究进行了系统性综述，构建了当代研究方法论的综合分类体系，分析了评估指标、工具和基准的趋势，揭示了缺乏可靠真实标签和标准化基准等关键挑战，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反编译已成为软件工程和安全性分析中的基础技术，并正通过现代机器学习方法的整合而向前发展。然而，该领域缺乏系统性的综述来梳理方法论、评估实践和开放挑战，这阻碍了研究社区对现状的理解和后续工作的开展。

Method: 作者对过去几十年发表的反编译研究进行了系统性文献综述，开发了一个全面的方法论分类体系，并进一步考察了用于评估最新方法的评估指标、工具和基准的趋势。

Result: 综述揭示了关键挑战，包括缺乏可靠的真实标签（ground truth）和缺少标准化基准，这些因素阻碍了不同方法之间进行严格的比较。

Conclusion: 基于综述发现，作者概述了未来的研究方向，旨在推动反编译领域在ML时代的进一步发展。

Abstract: Decompilation has become a foundational technique in software engineering and security analysis, and it is now advancing through the integration of modern machine learning (ML) approaches. This article presents a systematic review of decompilation studies published over the past decades and develops a comprehensive taxonomy of methodologies employed in contemporary research. We further examine trends in evaluation metrics, tools, and benchmarks used to assess state-of-the-art approaches. Our review reveals key challenges, such as the lack of reliable ground truth and the absence of standardized benchmarks, which hinder rigorous comparison. Finally, we outline future research directions.

</details>


### [20] [Evaluating and Preventing Security Smells in AI-Generated Ansible Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24962)
*Pandu Ranga Reddy Konala,Vimal Kumar,David Bainbridge,Junaid Haseeb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了16个AI模型生成Ansible基础设施代码的安全性，发现所有模型在无安全指导时生成的代码均存在安全气味且不合规。作者提出了基于扩展CO-STAR框架的提示工程方法，将Ansible最佳实践和CIS基准集成到提示中，实现安全问题的预防而非事后检测。应用该方法后，16个模型中有4个能生成合规代码，最优模型达到95%-100%的CIS合规率，较人类开发者（23%-43%）提升四倍，代码质量提升19%-49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码助手被广泛用于生成IaC（基础设施即代码），但此前的学术研究从未考察这些生成代码是否满足安全要求。由于基础设施代码中的安全气味会传播到部署后的系统，产生不安全且不可信的基础设施，因此评估和提升AI生成IaC的安全性具有重要现实意义。

Method: 研究评估了16个AI模型生成针对Apache Tomcat v10和MongoDB v7的Ansible角色，分析278个Ansible角色是否满足CIS基准要求。在此基础上，提出了一种将Ansible最佳实践和CIS基准整合进提示的方法，该方法基于扩展的CO-STAR框架，使安全气味预防发生在代码合成阶段而非部署后的检测阶段。

Result: 无安全指导时，16个AI模型全部产生含安全气味的代码，无法通过合规验证且不如人类开发者。应用所提方法后，4个模型能够生成合规代码，领先模型达到95%-100%的CIS合规率，是人类（23%-43%）的四倍，整体代码质量提升19%-49%。其余12个模型失败的原因是无法遵循多重约束的指令，而非无法生成代码。

Conclusion: 对于具备指令跟随能力的AI模型，该方法无需重新训练即可通过系统提示直接采用，为提升AI生成基础设施代码的安全性提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: AI coding assistants generate Infrastructure as Code, yet no work has examined whether this code meets security requirements. This matters because security smells in infrastructure code propagate to deployed systems, producing infrastructure that is insecure and untrustworthy. We evaluate 16 AI models generating Ansible roles for Apache Tomcat v10 and MongoDB v7, analysing 278 Ansible roles against CIS benchmarks. Without security guidance, all 16 AI models produced code containing security smells, resulting in vulnerable infrastructure that fails compliance verification and underperforms code written by human developers. We introduce an approach integrating Ansible best practices and CIS benchmarks into prompts through an extended CO-STAR framework, enabling security smell prevention during synthesis rather than detection after deployment. When this approach is applied, 4 out of 16 models generate compliant code, with the leading model achieving 95%-100% CIS compliance, a fourfold improvement over humans at 23%-43%, with overall code quality improving by 19%-49%. The remaining 12 models fail not because they cannot generate code but because they cannot follow instructions with multiple constraints. For capable models, the approach requires no retraining and can be adopted through system prompts.

</details>


### [21] [ARISMA: Guidelines for AI- and LLM-Assisted Systematic Reviews, Scoping Reviews, and Mapping Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25050)
*Mahyar Tourchi Moghaddam,Mina Alipour*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ARISMA（AI报告与集成标准，用于系统方法与分析），这是一个面向人工智能辅助证据综合（系统综述、范围综述、映射研究等）的端到端操作标准。ARISMA将AI定位为"受检查、受基准测试、被记录、可逆"的助手而非自主审稿人，核心原则是所有关键科学决策必须保持人类可解释、可审计和可问责。论文贡献了生命周期分类法、流程指导、审查管线分步建议、治理与溯源模型、工具支持框架、AI集成报告清单及验证矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工工作流难以应对日益增长的检索量、更新周期和综合需求，而AI/ML/LLM虽已进入综述实践的各个环节，但实证证据不均匀且不足以支持无约束自动化。现有标准（PRISMA 2020、PRISMA-S、PRISMA-ScR等）虽必要，但缺乏一个端到端操作标准来规范AI使用的适用性、验证方式、人类必须主导的决策以及AI参与的报告审计方式。

Method: ARISMA围绕"每个关键科学决策必须保持人类可解释、可审计、可问责"这一核心原则构建，提出：生命周期分类法、跨综述管线的流程指导和分步建议、治理与溯源模型、工具支持框架、AI集成报告清单、验证矩阵，并纳入法律、隐私、基础设施和可持续性考量。框架通过结构化专家咨询迭代精炼。

Result: 最终产出一套实用且可审计的负责任AI辅助证据综合指南，涵盖AI作为受检查、受基准测试、被记录和可逆助手的使用框架，并提供完整的生命周期分类法、报告清单和验证矩阵等工具。

Conclusion: ARISMA填补了现有综述报告标准中缺少AI集成端到端操作标准的空白，为AI在证据综合中的方法学适用性、验证要求、人类主导决策和可审计报告提供了系统化框架，是可实践、可审计的负责任AI辅助证据综合指南。

Abstract: Systematic reviews, scoping reviews, mapping studies, and related evidence syntheses are increasingly difficult to conduct with fully manual workflows as search volumes, update cycles, and synthesis requirements continue to expand. At the same time, artificial intelligence, machine learning, and large language models are rapidly entering review practice across query formulation, screening, extraction, categorization, appraisal support, and reporting. Yet the empirical evidence remains uneven, task-dependent, and insufficient to justify unconstrained automation. Existing standards such as PRISMA 2020, PRISMA-S, PRISMA-ScR, PRISMA-P, PRESS, and SWiM remain essential, but none provides an end-to-end operational standard for when AI use is methodologically appropriate, how it should be validated, which review decisions must remain human-led, and how AI involvement should be reported so that readers can audit it. This paper proposes ARISMA, an AI Reporting and Integration standard for Systematic Methods and Analysis. ARISMA treats AI as an inspected, benchmarked, logged, and reversible assistant rather than an autonomous reviewer. It is built around one governing principle: every consequential scientific decision must remain human-interpretable, human-auditable, and human-accountable. The paper contributes a lifecycle taxonomy, process guidance, stepwise recommendations across the review pipeline, a governance and provenance model, a tool-support framework, an AI-integrated reporting checklist, and a validation matrix. It also addresses legal, privacy, infrastructure, and sustainability considerations. The framework was iteratively refined through structured expert consultation. The result is a practical and auditable guideline for responsible AI-assisted evidence synthesis.

</details>


### [22] [Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25174)
*James C. Davis,Kelechi Kalu,Huiyun Peng,Parth V. Patil*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MAGE（基于模型的智能体软件工程）是一个框架和理论，旨在将商品化AI智能体的能力转化为可信赖的工程产出。它通过外部化最小有目的的表征（解决表征问题）并赋予已确定的义务以相应的权威（解决权威问题），借助约束、传感器、验证器和门控来构建受治理的工程环境。该框架源于一个纵向案例，并经过六个独立工业案例的完善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体增加了实现能力，但不会自动使项目意图、系统结构或验收证据变得明确。随着实现变得充裕而工程判断变得稀缺，关键工作转向选择有用的抽象、产生证据和确定验收标准。现有工作流（更大的提示、仓库检索、逐变更审查）只能部分弥补这一差距，仍需智能体和工程师反复重构关键属性。

Method: MAGE框架从两个核心问题入手：一是表征问题——外部化回答工程问题所需的最小有目的表征；二是权威问题——通过约束、传感器、验证器和门控，赋予已确定的义务以相应的权威。框架将不确定的意图保持开放，将反复发生的重构和判断转化为可继承的持久工程结构。开发过程基于一个纵向案例，并通过六个独立报告的工业案例进行提炼和完善。

Result: 通过纵向案例和六个工业案例的验证，MAGE展示了外部化知识、受限行动、独立评估和保留人类权威如何组合成一个受治理的工程环境，并提出了判断该环境何时能将军商品智能转化为持久工程进展的测试方法。

Conclusion: MAGE为工程智能体提供了一种不同于单纯扩大提示或增加检索的替代路径：通过将判断和重构外部化为持久的工程结构，使工程团队能以受治理的方式利用商品化AI能力，同时保留人类在不确定性事务上的权威。

Abstract: Coding agents increase implementation capacity without automatically making project intent, system structure, or acceptance evidence explicit. As implementation becomes abundant relative to engineering judgment, the scarce work shifts toward choosing useful abstractions, producing evidence, and determining which obligations govern acceptance. Existing workflows address parts of this gap through larger prompts, repository retrieval, or perchange review, but still require agents and engineers to reconstruct consequential properties.
  As an alternative, we present Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering (MAGE). MAGE is a framework and a theory for building trustworthy autonomy from commodity intelligence. MAGE addresses a representation problem and an authority problem: it externalizes the smallest purposeful representation needed to answer an engineering question, then gives settled obligations proportionate authority through constraints, sensors, validators, and gates. It keeps uncertain intent open and turns recurring reconstruction and judgment into durable engineering structure that later work can inherit. We developed MAGE from a longitudinal case and refined it through six independently reported industrial accounts. Across these sources, MAGE explains how externalized knowledge, bounded action, independent evaluation, and retained human authority can compose into a governed engineering environment, and proposes tests of when that environment turns commodity intelligence into durable engineering progress.

</details>


### [23] [SPECMINE: A Large-Scale Corpus of Spec-Driven Development Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25202)
*Shyam Agarwal,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPECMINE，一个大规模语料库，用于研究GitHub公共仓库中的Spec-Driven Development（SDD）实践。该语料库通过两种普查方式（广泛普查覆盖470,795份spec文件，Kiro普查覆盖98,574份文件），并配合PR变更集和类型化引用索引，首次实现在大规模尺度上研究AI代理时代的软件规范编写方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spec-Driven Development（SDD）作为一种新兴实践，自2025年以来涌现了大量工具（如GitHub Spec Kit、OpenSpec、AWS Kiro等），但这些工具产生的规范工件从未被大规模研究过。此外，"规范如何变成代码"这一核心问题仍然是开放的，缺乏系统性的数据支持。

Method: 本文构建了SPECMINE语料库，包含两种普查：（1）广泛普查：覆盖470,795个spec.md/specs.md文件，涉及73,030个仓库，归因于17个具名工具；（2）Kiro普查：针对Kiro独有的requirements/design/tasks布局，覆盖98,574个文件、12,910个仓库。每个spec都配有完整仓库元数据、完整提交历史和解析后的文档结构。此外，针对11个工具，扫描了所有触及spec的PR（至少10星仓库），捕获581个仓库中的5,992个PR及其变更集；同时构建了包含2,421,323个类型化引用（128万指向代码文件，86.3万指向兄弟文档，15.2万指向PR等）的全普查索引。

Result: SPECMINE成功构建了一个大规模、多维度的SDD语料库，包含广泛的spec文件普查、Kiro专用普查、PR级变更集分析以及类型化引用索引，使最简单的SDD工作流（spec和实现在同一PR中一起变化）可以直接观察，并提供了从spec到代码的第二条独立链接。

Conclusion: SPECMINE首次为社区提供了在AI代理时代研究软件规范编写方式的大规模数据基础，使得研究者能够系统性地回答"规范如何变成代码"等关键问题。

Abstract: Spec-Driven Development (SDD) is a fast-emerging practice in which a structured natural-language specification, written by a developer, or (more often) drafted by an AI tool and then curated by the developer, drives an AI coding agent's implementation. A wave of tooling (GitHub Spec Kit [3], OpenSpec [4], AWS Kiro [5], and dozens of others) has appeared since 2025, yet the artifacts these tools produce have never been studied at scale. We present SPECMINE, a corpus that captures SDD in public GitHub repositories through two censuses: a broad census of spec.md/specs.md files covering most tools (470,795 files across 73,030 repositories, attributed to 17 named tools), and a Kiro census of its distinct requirements/design/tasks layout (98,574 files across 12,910 repositories). Each spec is enriched with full repository metadata, complete commit history, and parsed document structure. How a spec becomes code is itself an open question, so for 11 tools we sweep every pull request that touches a spec in their repositories with at least ten stars, capturing 5,992 such PRs across 581 repositories with their changesets. That makes the simplest workflow, spec and implementation changing together in one PR, directly observable, and a census-wide index of 2,421,323 typed references (1.28M to code files, 863k to sibling documents, 152k to PRs, 62k refs, 43k branches, 22k issues) gives a second, independent link from spec to code. SPECMINE lets the community study, for the first time, how software is specified in the age of AI agents.

</details>


### [24] [A Few Pages of Markdown: Committed AI Configuration and Lower Quality Cost after Coding-Agent Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25241)
*Yegor Denisov-Blanch,Shyam Agarwal,Pavel Azaletskiy,Hao He,Rylan Schaeffer,Brando Miranda,Bogdan Vasilescu,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RAMP（仓库AI成熟度画像），一个四级累积成熟度模型，用于根据团队提交的版本控制制品来配置AI工具。基于441个仓库的实证研究发现，RAMP层级构成累积量表，独立人工标注复现率达97%。在按成熟度分层的分析中，AI代理无论成熟度如何都加速开发（多28-38%提交），但质量表现分化：未提交AI配置的代理优先仓库认知复杂度增加约两倍（+53%对+27%），静态分析告警增加1.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于编码代理的研究仅报告采用者的平均效应，掩盖了团队之间的巨大差异。作者希望提供一个可复用的工具，基于版本控制中的实际配置制品来刻画团队使用AI工具的成熟度，从而区分不同团队在使用AI代理时的开发速度和代码质量差异。

Method: 引入RAMP四级累积成熟度模型：从行为规则和编码标准，到命名代理定义，再到多代理编排。基于441个仓库的版本控制制品进行评估，验证层级的行为作为累积量表，并通过独立人工标注进行验证。随后在每一成熟度层级内重新估计已有的代理采用面板数据，比较按成熟度分层的开发速度与代码质量指标。

Result: RAMP层级表现出累积量表特征，人工标注复现率97%。73.8%的制品提交后从未修改（一次性配置）。各成熟度层级下代理都加速开发（28-38%更多提交），但在代理优先仓库中，未提交AI配置的仓库认知复杂度增长约为已配置仓库的两倍（+53% vs +27%），静态分析告警增加1.7倍。

Conclusion: AI代理对开发速度和代码质量的影响因团队成熟度而异。由于成熟度是观测性变量，工程纪律或模型能力等关联因素可能部分解释差异，研究结果具有假设生成性质。作者发布RAMP作为可复用工具供社区使用。

Abstract: Coding agents increase development velocity but also technical debt. Prior work reports only average effects across adopters, hiding wide differences between teams. We introduce RAMP (Repository AI Maturity Profile), a four-level cumulative maturity model grounded in version-controlled artifacts that teams commit to configure AI tools. RAMP runs from behavioral rules and coding standards through named agent definitions to multi-agent orchestration, with observed practice concentrated in the first three levels. Across 441 repositories the levels behave as a cumulative scale, and independent human annotation reproduces RAMP's repository-level labels on 97% of a held-out sample. Adoption is cumulative, forward-only, and set-and-forget: 73.8% of artifacts are committed once and never modified. Re-estimating an existing agent-adoption panel within each stratum, agents accelerate development regardless of maturity (28-38% more commits), but quality diverges: among agent-first repositories, where the contrast is identified, those without committed AI configuration show roughly twice the increase in cognitive complexity (+53% versus +27%) and 1.7x the increase in static-analysis warnings. Because maturity is observational, correlated engineering discipline or model capability may explain part of the gap; we present these findings as hypothesis-generating and release RAMP as a reusable instrument.

</details>


### [25] [Metis: Typed Runtime Mediation for Tool-Using Software Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25322)
*Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Metis，一个多提供商运行时系统，将提供商流转换为类型化事件后再执行外部操作，并通过明确的权限决策、干扰分类、终端结果和生命周期转换机制来实现可检查的代理执行路径。实验表明其在30个匹配的真实I/O对上将中位延迟从25.958ms降至14.146ms，并在权限路由、子边界控制和追踪闭合等方面取得了积极结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件代理将概率模型的输出连接到实际操作（如修改仓库、流程、网络和图形应用），这一过程缺乏对执行路径的明确性和可检查性。需要一个运行时系统，使权限决策、干扰分类、终端结果和生命周期转换变得显式化和可审查，以确保代理调用的外部效应受到可靠控制。

Method: Metis采用多提供商架构，在处理调用到达外部效应之前，将提供商流转换为类型化事件。其执行路径显式化权限决策、干扰类别、终端结果和生命周期转换。评估方法包括：（1）在30个匹配的真实I/O对上对比强制序列化与四类仲裁的中位延迟；（2）构建十案例故障矩阵暴露重复标识符与回滚限制；（3）通过子边界消融实验验证完整门禁加注册表条件对未授权效应的阻断能力；（4）使用仅决定权限预言机跨五条调用路由匹配测试；（5）五种模型条件完成固定读取标记协议的测试。

Result: 30个匹配真实I/O对中，四类仲裁将中位延迟从25.958ms（强制序列化）降至14.146ms，平均配对差异为-12.295ms（95%自举区间[-12.968, -11.694]），所有配对中仲裁均更快。十案例故障矩阵暴露了重复标识符和回滚限制。完整门禁加注册表条件成功阻断声明的未授权效应并隐藏全部五个逃逸工具，移除两项保护后结果反转。权限预言机跨五条调用路由匹配全部十个声明案例，五种模型条件在固定读取标记协议中均3/3通过。

Conclusion: Metis的结果支持在调度、权限路由、子权威和提供商验证追踪闭合方面的有界声明。但作者明确指出，该工作不建立模型能力、语义安全性、回滚能力或对其他运行时的优越性。研究边界限定明确，为后续工作留下了安全语义和回滚机制等方面的改进空间。

Abstract: Software agents connect probabilistic model output to operations that change repositories, processes, networks, and graphical applications. We present Metis, a multi-provider runtime that converts provider streams into typed events before admitted calls reach external effects. Its execution path makes permission decisions, interference classes, terminal results, and lifecycle transitions explicit and inspectable. We evaluate these mechanisms on frozen source artifacts. Across 30 matched real-I/O pairs, four-class mediation reduced median elapsed time from 25.958 ms under forced serialization to 14.146 ms. The mean paired difference was -12.295 ms (95% bootstrap interval [-12.968, -11.694]), with mediation faster in all pairs. A ten-case fault matrix exposed duplicate-identifier and rollback limits. In a child-boundary ablation, the full gate-plus-registry condition blocked the declared unauthorized effect and hid all five escape tools. Removing both protections reversed both observations. A decision-only permission oracle matched all ten declared cases across five invocation routes. Five model conditions also completed a fixed Read-marker protocol in 3/3 trials each. These results support bounded claims about dispatch, permission routing, child authority, and provider-valid trace closure. They do not establish model competence, semantic safety, rollback, or superiority over another runtime.

</details>


### [26] [Point-in-Time Audit Before Alpha: Public-Archive Availability and a Negative Matched-Budget Study on BTC Perpetual Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25348)
*Baocheng Zeng,Jinhao Yang,Peilin Han,Kangnan He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对币安BTCUSDT USD-M永续合约的公开加密货币档案进行了系统性审计，发现无缺口五分钟数据要求无法满足（最长未修复间隙304.57天），修订后保留727个完整UTC日。审计器在80个冻结模板上检测出40/40违规并拒绝0/40合法模板，10条零信号路径平均误通过率从0.2910降至0.0625。受审计的自适应代理与随机搜索持平，一次历史留出集上所有运行IC为正但净Sharpe为负，最终报告了有范围的负面结果而非盈利或代理优越性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开加密货币档案可能看似可用，但因子研究要求每个决策时点的观测值可获取且可执行。作者旨在审计这些公开数据的可靠性，区分提案式审计与确定性审计、评估和留出访问，以避免数据可用性偏差。

Method: 采用事件时间、发布时间和可用性时间三个维度审计币安BTCUSDT永续合约数据，区分提案与确定性审计/评估/留出访问。先设定无缺口五分钟数据要求，失败后修订核心流为trade/mark/index/realized funding，并将open interest设为可选。使用80个冻结已知规则模板测试审计器，并在10条零信号路径上比较完全审计与未经审计的误通过率。

Result: 初始无缺口要求失败（最长未修复交叉间隙304.57天）；修订后保留727个完整UTC日，形成436/145/146天的训练/验证/历史留出划分。审计器检测出40/40违规模板、拒绝0/40合法模板；完全审计将平均误通过率从0.2910降至0.0625。受审计自适应代理未证明优于随机搜索，历史留出集所有运行IC为正但净Sharpe为负。

Conclusion: 公开币安永续期货数据在严格无缺口要求下不可用，即使修订后也只能支持有限时间跨度。整体结果为一个范围限定的负面结论：不应声称盈利性或代理优越性，而是强调公开加密货币档案审计的重要性及"数据看似可用"与实际可执行性之间的差距。

Abstract: Public cryptocurrency archives may appear usable when files exist, although factor research requires observations available and executable at each decision time. We audit public Binance BTCUSDT USD-M perpetual-futures data using event, publication, and availability times and separate proposal from deterministic auditing, evaluation, and holdout access. An initial gapless five-minute requirement for trade, mark, index, and open interest failed: the longest unrepaired intersection was 304.5729166666667 days. A disclosed revision made trade, mark, index, and realized funding the core streams and made open interest optional because its publication time was unverified. The revised mask retained 727 complete UTC days and supported a 436/145/146-day train, validation, and historical-holdout split. On 80 frozen known-rule templates, the auditor detected 40/40 violations and rejected 0/40 legal templates. Across ten null-signal paths, full auditing reduced mean false passes from 0.2910 to 0.0625. Under matched valid-candidate budgets, the audited adaptive agent tied random search and did not establish superiority. In the one-time historical holdout, all evaluated runs had positive IC but negative net Sharpe under primary costs. We therefore report a scoped negative result rather than a profitability or agent-superiority claim.

</details>


### [27] [Retry Amplification in Distributed Systems: A Systematic Analysis of Retry Policies and Their Role in Cascading Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25403)
*Rishabh Mehan,Jasmit Kaur Saluja*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出重试放大因子（RAF）指标量化分布式系统中重试策略产生的额外请求量。通过对200个开源Python微服务项目的研究，发现显式重试逻辑占比11.5%（实际流行率约41%），60.9%的项目存在无退避配置，仅1/113的生产配置随机化延迟。模拟实验表明，在相关故障下，朴素标准重试策略将成功率从55.4%降至41.5%。作者提出自适应重试预算（ARB），证明预算约束下的重试能保持接近无重试基线的成功率。结论是重试行为应作为系统级属性设计，而非在各调用点局部配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统中的重试机制虽然被广泛使用，但各层级并发重试的集体行为尚未被充分理解。现有指导主要基于单个客户端视角，缺乏对重试策略在部分故障场景下系统性影响的量化分析，需要引入指标和评估方法来揭示重试策略的全局副作用。

Method: （1）提出重试放大因子（RAF）指标量化重试策略产生的额外请求量；（2）扫描200个开源Python微服务项目，检测显式重试逻辑并审计误报率；（3）对检测到的重试配置进行人工验证（退避策略、延迟随机化等）；（4）使用模拟实验（n=100次/策略）评估不同重试策略在部分故障下的表现，特别关注相关故障场景；（5）归纳五种重试反模式，并提出自适应重试预算（ARB）机制。

Result: 显式重试逻辑检出率为11.5%，校正后真实流行率约41%；60.9%的重试配置无退避机制，仅1/113配置随机化延迟。模拟显示，在相关故障下朴素标准重试将成功率从55.4%（无重试基线）降至41.5%。自适应重试预算在保持接近基线成功率的同时，仍能恢复瞬时故障。

Conclusion: 重试策略不应仅作为各调用点的局部配置来设计，而应考虑为系统级属性。无节制的重试在有相关故障时不仅无益反而有害，预算约束机制是更稳健的替代方案。

Abstract: Retry mechanisms are a standard component of resilient distributed systems, but their collective behavior, when every tier in a call path retries concurrently, is less well understood than the per-client guidance that produced them. This paper introduces the retry amplification factor (RAF), a metric quantifying the additional request volume that retry policies generate during partial failures. In a study of 200 open-source Python microservice projects, explicit retry logic is detected in 11.5%, and an audit of our own false negatives places true prevalence near 41%. Among the projects detected, 60.9% contain at least one configuration without backoff, and after manual verification exactly one of 113 production configurations randomizes its delay. We then evaluate these policies in simulation (n = 100 trials per strategy). Under correlated failures, a naive standard retry policy reduces the success rate from 55.4% to 41.5% relative to performing no retries at all. We catalog five recurring anti-patterns, propose Adaptive Retry Budgeting (ARB), and show that budget-constrained retries maintain success rates close to the no-retry baseline while still recovering from transient faults. These results indicate that retry behavior should be designed as a system-level property rather than configured locally at each call site.

</details>


### [28] [RotDroid: Cross-Orientation State Equivalence Testing for Detecting GUI Rotation Bugs in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25425)
*Mengdi Qin,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出RotDroid框架，通过跨方向状态等价性检测Android应用中的GUI旋转缺陷，并构建RotBench数据集和微调视觉语言模型RotVL作为测试预言机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕旋转是Android应用的基本交互，但常引入非崩溃功能性缺陷（如布局不一致和状态丢失），这些缺陷难以自动检测。核心挑战在于缺乏有效的测试预言机来检查横竖屏之间的状态等价性。

Method: 1) RotDroid生成并变异状态保持动作序列（SPS）以构造跨取向语义等价的GUI状态；2) 构建RotBench数据集（横竖屏GUI状态配对）用于预言机检查；3) 微调视觉语言模型RotVL用于等价性判定。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上，RotVL优于现有最先进模型；在同等预算下RotDroid比现有技术检测到更多旋转引发的缺陷；在大型开源和闭源应用研究中报告了94个先前未知的bug，其中47个已获开发者确认或修复。

Conclusion: RotDroid通过跨方向状态等价性检查有效解决了旋转缺陷检测中测试预言机缺失的问题，并在真实应用中展示了实际有效性。

Abstract: Screen rotation is a fundamental interaction in Android applications, but it often introduces non-crashing functional failures (NCFs), such as layout inconsistencies and state loss, which are difficult to detect automatically. A key challenge is the lack of effective test oracles for checking cross-orientation state equivalence between portrait and landscape views. We propose RotDroid, a testing framework for detecting GUI rotation bugs via cross-orientation state equivalence. RotDroid generates and mutates State-Preserving action Sequences (SPS) to construct semantically equivalent GUI states across orientations. To support reliable oracle checking, we build RotBench, a dataset of paired portrait-landscape GUI states, and develop RotVL, a vision-language model fine-tuned for equivalence checking. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that RotVL outperforms state-of-the-art models, and RotDroid detects more rotation-induced failures than existing techniques under equal budgets. In large-scale studies on open- and closed-source apps, RotDroid reports 94 previously unknown bugs, with 47 confirmed or fixed by developers, demonstrating its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [29] [A Hybrid Usability Approach for Rating Evaluation of M-Commerce Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25550)
*Ahmad Ibtisam,Bilal Khan,Arshad Ali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对移动商务（M-commerce）应用的混合可用性模型，用于预测应用评分。模型包含8个可用性因素（可学习性、一致性、人为因素、沟通性、有效性、可操作性、效率、满意度），通过对5个知名M-commerce应用（Daraz、Shophive、Home Shopping、Symbios、Yayvo）各40名用户收集数据，采用Forward Stepwise多元线性回归构建评分预测模型，并使用PRED(x)和K-fold方法进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用的成功取决于其有用性，而评分是衡量有用性的重要指标。现有可用性模型缺乏针对M-commerce应用评分预测的系统性评估，需要探索哪些可用性因素对应用评分贡献显著，以建立更准确的评分预测框架。

Method: 首先调研现有可用性模型及因素标准；其次从5个M-commerce应用中收集用户数据（每个应用40名用户）；然后构建包含8个因素的初始混合可用性模型；接着使用Forward Stepwise多元线性回归分析各因素下的每个标准来确定关键预测因子；最后采用PRED(x)和K-fold交叉验证评估模型性能。

Result: 提出了基于Forward Stepwise多元线性回归的评分预测模型，通过分析混合可用性模型的各因素标准来识别对评分有显著贡献的可用性因素，模型经PRED(x)和K-fold技术验证具有良好的预测能力。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种混合可用性模型及基于回归的评分预测方法，有助于识别影响M-commerce应用评分的关键可用性因素，为应用开发者改进产品提供理论依据。

Abstract: The success of any mobile application relies on its usefulness and rating is considered as an important measure in this regard. This research work focuses on identifying usability factors, which contribute significantly towards the rating of M-commerce apps. This work intends to explore existing usability models consisting of different factors along with a set of criteria and evaluate in terms of rating estimation by considering 5 well-known mobile applications, namely (i) daraz, (ii) shophive, (iii) home shopping, (iv) Symbios. (v) yayvo. Then, this work provides a hybrid usability model for rating prediction of M-commerce applications. The initial hybrid usability model comprises of (i) learnability, (ii) consistency, (iii) human factors,(iv)communicativeness,(v)effectiveness, (vi) Operability, (vii) efficiency, (viii) satisfaction. Each factor consists of some criteria. Keeping in view the factors of hybrid usability model, the data was collected from 40 users for each application. Furthermore, Forward Stepwise Multiple Linear Regression based rating prediction model is suggested by analyzing each criterion of all factors of hybrid usability model. Finally, the model is assessed and validated by using PRED(x) and K-fold techniques.

</details>


### [30] [From General Agents to RCA Experts: A Self-Evolving Harness for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25661)
*Haiyu Huang,Jiewei Lyu,Zhihan Jiang,Jinyang Liu,Xiao He,Tieying Zhang,Wu Xiang,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OpsHarness，一种自进化的根因分析（RCA）工具框架。其核心观点是：现代通用LLM代理（如Codex/Claude Code）的诊断能力已超过从零构建的专用RCA代理，因此RCA应聚焦于构建代理外部"harness"适配层而非重建代理。OpsHarness通过分层运维知识、想法卡片工具库、以及双门验证机制，将诊断经验转化为可复用的专业知识，在公共基准和工业部署中达到59.0%的top-1准确率，相对裸通用代理提升63.4%，相对基线RCA代理提升4.02倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based RCA存在两条路径：直接使用通用代理或从零构建专用RCA代理。定量研究发现前者准确率已超越后者，但仍达不到生产需求，且差距主要来自代理通用能力之外的外部适配层（harness）。因此需要聚焦构建自进化的harness，复用现代代理的强通用能力而非重复造轮子。

Method: OpsHarness采用双平面架构：(1) 数据平面：结合分层运维知识与"想法卡片"（idea-card）工具库，沉淀可复用的诊断知识；(2) 控制平面：协调设置、诊断、进化与验证四个环节。进化过程中，通过对比成功与失败的诊断轨迹，将证据转化为原子化改进提案，并仅通过双门验证机制（防过拟合与回归）后才接纳更新。

Result: 在两个公共基准和一个工业部署中，OpsHarness达到59.0%的top-1准确率，相比裸通用代理提升63.4%，相比基线RCA代理提升4.02倍。

Conclusion: LLM-based RCA的未来方向应聚焦于可自进化的外部harness，而非从零构建专用代理。通过将历史诊断经验转化为系统特定知识并持续进化，可以在复用现代通用代理能力的基础上显著提升RCA的准确率和生产适用性。

Abstract: Automated root cause analysis (RCA) with large language models (LLMs) has drawn growing attention. Today, SREs typically automate RCA with LLMs in one of two ways: directly using a general-purpose agent (e.g., Codex or Claude Code) for diagnosis, or building a specialized RCA agent from scratch. As mainstream general agents grow more capable and iterate quickly, our quantitative study finds that the former now often surpasses the latter. Its accuracy, however, still falls short of production needs, and this gap stems mainly from the external adaptation layer outside the agent's general capabilities, namely the harness. We therefore argue that LLM-based RCA should focus on this external harness, reusing the strong general capabilities of a modern agent rather than rebuilding an agent from scratch. A key capability of such a harness is to self-evolve, accumulating system-specific experience from past diagnoses so that it gets better the more it is used. We introduce OpsHarness, a self-evolving RCA harness that turns diagnosis experience into reusable expertise. Its data plane combines layered operational knowledge with an idea-card tool library, while its control plane coordinates setup, diagnosis, evolution, and verification. During evolution, OpsHarness contrasts successful and failed trajectories, converts their evidence into atomic proposals, and admits updates only through a dual-gate verification process designed to prevent overfitting and regression. Across two public benchmarks and an industrial deployment, OpsHarness achieves 59.0\% top-1 accuracy, improving over a bare general agent by 63.4\% and over baseline RCA agents by 4.02$\times$.

</details>


### [31] [Predicting Struggling Students in CS1 Programming Using Keystroke-Level Editing Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25769)
*Yasuyo Kofune,Kazumasa Shimari,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文利用CodeBench平台的击键级日志，研究在CS1编程练习中早期识别陷入困境学生的可行性。实验表明，结合执行日志和击键编辑特征（Combined）在最早阶段预测效果最佳（AUROC 0.674），击键级特征比仅用执行日志特征更具预测力，且最早阶段预测信号最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一些学生在编程练习结束时仍无法得出正确答案，而通过成绩或最终结果发现这一点时为时已晚，错过了教师及时干预帮助学生恢复学习的机会。因此需要探索能否通过更细粒度的日志数据（击键级）早期识别这些学生，以便及时提供支持。

Method: 使用CodeBench平台记录代码编辑过程的击键级日志、执行日志和提交日志；将学生分为BT（最终突破）和FS（完全卡住）两组；比较三种特征配置：仅执行日志特征（ExecOnly）、仅CodeMirror击键特征（CMOnly）、两者组合（Combined）；在每次练习的连续提交阶段进行预测评估，使用2019-1学期507名学生的数据进行实验。

Result: 在最早阶段，CMOnly优于ExecOnly（AUROC 0.654 vs 0.575），Combined相比ExecOnly提升+0.098（AUROC 0.674）。所有配置中，最早阶段的预测信号最强。

Conclusion: 练习开始阶段的行为信号已包含关于学生最终能否解决问题的有用线索；击键级编辑日志相比仅执行日志，能为早期优先干预提供额外的预测价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates the feasibility of early detection of struggling students during CS1 programming exercises using keystroke-level logs. Some students fail to reach a correct solution before the exercise ends, and by the time this becomes apparent from grades or final outcomes, the opportunity for timely instructor support aimed at helping them recover may have passed. We use data from the CodeBench platform, which records real-time code editing events at the keystroke level, alongside execution and submission logs. We define two outcome groups: Breakthrough (BT) students, whose prior submissions all receive 0% and whose final submission achieves full credit, and Fully Stuck (FS) students, whose submissions all receive 0% without reaching a correct solution. To examine this feasibility, we focus on two questions: (RQ1) whether adding keystroke-level editing features improves the prediction of FS students over execution-log features alone, and (RQ2) at which stage BT and FS students can be predicted most accurately. Experiments on the 2019-1 semester of the CodeBench dataset, comprising 507 students, compare three feature configurations: execution-based features (ExecOnly), CodeMirror-based features (CMOnly), and their combination (Combined). We evaluate prediction across successive submission-based stages during each exercise. In the earliest stage, CMOnly outperforms ExecOnly (AUROC 0.654 vs. 0.575), and Combined further improves over ExecOnly by +0.098 (AUROC 0.674). Across all configurations, the earliest stage yielded the strongest predictive signal. These findings indicate that behavioral signals present at the very start of an exercise contain useful clues about whether a student will ultimately solve the problem, and that keystroke-level editing logs provide additional value for early prioritization beyond execution logs alone.

</details>


### [32] [Beyond the Editing Canvas: Evidence Divergence in OOXML-to-LLM Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25880)
*Side Liu,Jiangpeng Liu,Jinwen Xin,Guojun Peng,Jiang Ming*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示OOXML文档在Microsoft Office与LLM提取管道之间存在"语义分歧"：同一规范合法的OOXML文件在Office和LLM提取时产生不同证据视图（称为"证据分叉"），导致LLM可能消费到Office画布中未显示的隐藏信息。研究确认了21种证据分叉机制，横跨Excel、Word和PowerPoint，多个主流LLM API和聊天机器人在20/21种机制中会暴露这些"陷阱"信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM管道越来越多地直接将OOXML文档（Word/Excel/PowerPoint）作为金融、合规和检索增强工作流中的一等证据，隐含假设模型消费的证据与Microsoft Office编辑画布中显示的内容一致。作者发现这一假设可能失败：同一OOXML文件在Office中显示的视图与为LLM提取的视图可以不同，每种视图都被各自的消费者视为权威，形成"多重真值"问题，威胁LLM管道的语义完整性和可信度。

Method: 作者系统地遍历和挖掘OOXML规范，确认了跨Excel、Word和PowerPoint的21种证据分叉，涵盖视图构建的六个维度。构建了包含13种工具的提取面板、4种原生摄取LLM API和7种网络聊天机器人的测试集。每个测试文档包含一个"陷阱"：一个通过提取可见但不在Office中显示的任务相关事实。此外还调查了16个流行的开源LLM项目源码，分析其默认OOXML摄取路径。

Result: 21种证据分叉均在提取面板的至少一个工具中发生；4种原生摄取LLM API在48-76%的试验中返回陷阱信息；21种机制中有20种被至少一个接口（共11个LLM接口）成功触发；暴露程度受上游摄取路径和提取器配置的影响；16个流行开源LLM项目中默认OOXML摄取路径集中于受影响的提取器家族。

Conclusion: OOXML到LLM的管道存在系统性语义完整性缺陷，"证据分叉"导致模型可能基于Office用户从未看到的隐藏事实进行推理。摄取契约（ingestion contract）很少明确声明哪个视图和语义角色会成为模型证据，也不保留证据的派生方式。这一发现对依赖Office文档作为证据的金融、合规和RAG工作流构成重大信任风险，需要在OOXML摄取路径中建立更严格的视图一致性和溯源机制。

Abstract: LLM pipelines increasingly ingest Office Open XML (OOXML) documents (Word, Excel, and PowerPoint files) as first-class evidence in financial, compliance, and retrieval-augmented workflows, implicitly assuming semantic integrity: that the evidence consumed by the model matches the content shown in the Microsoft Office suite editing canvas. We show that this assumption can fail in OOXML-to-LLM pipelines. The same specification-valid OOXML file can yield one evidentiary view in Microsoft Office and another when extracted for an LLM. Each view is treated as authoritative by its consumer, a condition we call plural ground truth. The ingestion contract rarely states which view and semantic roles become model evidence or preserves how that evidence was derived. We call the specification-grounded OOXML constructions that induce such divergence evidence forks.
  We systematically traverse and mine the OOXML specification and confirm 21 evidence forks across Excel, Word, and PowerPoint, spanning six dimensions of view construction. All 13 tools in our extraction panel emit evidence from at least one fork. We test four native-ingestion LLM APIs and seven web chatbots. Each test document carries a trap: a task-relevant fact exposed by extraction but not shown in Office. Across this 21-mechanism evaluation, the four APIs return the trap in 48--76% of trials. For 20 of 21 mechanisms, at least one of the eleven interfaces returns the trap. Our measurements further show that exposure is shaped upstream of the model by the ingestion path and extractor configuration. A source-level survey of sixteen popular open-source LLM projects further shows that default OOXML ingestion paths concentrate on affected extractor families.

</details>


### [33] [Answer Is Cheap, Show Me the Evidence! Augmenting Automated Vulnerability Assessment with Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25905)
*Shengyi Pan,Zelong Zheng,Jiayuan Zhou,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对软件漏洞评估任务，提出EAVA框架，利用大语言模型代理处理富文本和项目上下文信息，并通过两阶段训练（监督指令微调+强化学习）构建专用评估模型，同时检索相似历史漏洞作为补充证据。在新增的SVR数据集上超越最强基线5.3%-35.2%，用户研究验证了其证据提供的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化软件漏洞评估方法往往忽略富文本信息（如截图、代码片段）和漏洞项目上下文，且只关注预测准确率而不提供解释或支持性证据，限制了实际应用中分析人员验证不可靠预测的能力。

Method: EAVA框架包含三个核心组件：1）专门的LLM智能体处理富文本内容和项目信息；2）两阶段训练流水线构建专用评估模型——先通过监督指令微调注入领域知识（基于自动标注的推理轨迹），再用强化学习提升内在推理能力；3）检索相似历史漏洞作为补充证据。

Result: 在新建的SVR数据集上，EAVA在多个指标上超越最强基线5.3%至35.2%；消融实验证实了评估专用模型训练和信息增强的有效性；安全专家的用户研究验证了EAVA提供的证据在真实软件漏洞评估中具有实用价值。

Conclusion: EAVA通过LLM智能体感知富文本和项目上下文，结合两阶段训练和相似漏洞检索，在提升漏洞评估性能的同时提供可解释的支持性证据，显著增强了自动化漏洞评估的实际可用性。

Abstract: Software vulnerability (SV) assessment helps prioritize remediation by characterizing reported vulnerabilities. Existing
  automated methods predict assessment results from SV reports (SVRs), but often overlook information in rich text, such as
  screenshots and code snippets, as well as contextual information about vulnerable projects. They also focus on prediction
  accuracy without providing explanations or supporting evidence, limiting their practical use when analysts must validate
  imperfect predictions. We propose EAVA, a framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to assess SVs and provide
  supporting evidence. EAVA employs specialized LLM agents to process rich-text content and project information, and builds a
  dedicated assessment model through a two-stage training pipeline. It first uses supervised instruction tuning on automatically
  annotated reasoning trajectories to inject domain knowledge, and then applies reinforcement learning to improve intrinsic
  reasoning. EAVA also retrieves similar historical vulnerabilities as supplementary evidence. Experiments on a newly collected
  SVR dataset show that EAVA outperforms the strongest baseline by 5.3 to 35.2 percent across multiple metrics. Ablation studies
  confirm the effectiveness of assessment-specific model training and information enrichment. A user study with security experts
  further demonstrates that the evidence provided by EAVA is useful and practical for real-world SV assessment.

</details>


### [34] [XREPOTEST: Benchmarking Multilingual Repository-Level Unit Test Generation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25939)
*Dung Le Quang,Dong Cao Van,Nam Le Hai,Linh Ngo Van,Anh M. T. Bui,Phuong T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出XREPOTEST，一个多语言仓库级单元测试生成基准，涵盖Rust、Go、Julia、PHP和Ruby五种语言，在容器化真实仓库约束下评估14个SOTA LLM，并提出Invocation Rate（IR）指标衡量测试是否真正调用目标功能，揭示独立设置与仓库级性能之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM单元测试生成的评估大多依赖独立（standalone）设置和少量编程语言，高估了模型在真实软件环境中的就绪程度。需要一个更真实、更广泛的多语言仓库级基准来准确评估LLM的实际能力。

Method: 构建覆盖五种未充分探索语言（Rust、Go、Julia、PHP、Ruby）的多语言仓库级基准XREPOTEST；采用容器化执行框架模拟真实仓库约束；设计多种上下文增强策略（文件级、基于LSP、基于检索）；除标准指标（测试通过率、覆盖率）外，提出Invocation Rate（IR）新指标评估生成测试是否有效调用目标功能。

Result: 对14个SOTA LLM（包括Claude 4.5、GPT-5.2、DeepSeek V4-Pro和Qwen系列）的实验表明，独立设置与仓库级性能之间存在显著差距，且更丰富的上下文与测试可靠性之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: XREPOTEST作为一个具有挑战性和信息量的基准，可推动在真实软件环境中可扩展且稳健的单元测试生成研究。数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for automated unit test generation, but existing evaluations largely rely on standalone settings and a narrow set of programming languages, overestimating real-world readiness. We introduce XREPOTEST, a multilingual repository-level benchmark for unit test generation spanning five underexplored languages: Rust, Go, Julia, PHP, and Ruby. XREPOTEST evaluates tests under realistic repository constraints using a containerized execution framework and multiple context augmentation strategies, including file-level, LSP-based, and retrieval-based context. Beyond standard metrics such as test pass rate and coverage, we propose Invocation Rate (IR) to assess whether generated tests meaningfully exercise the intended functionality. Experiments with 14 state-of-the-art LLMs, including Claude 4.5, GPT-5.2, DeepSeek V4-Pro, and Qwen families, reveal a substantial gap between standalone and repository-level performance, as well as trade-offs between richer context and test reliability. Overall, XREPOTEST provides a challenging and informative benchmark to advance scalable and robust unit test generation in realistic software environments. The dataset and code are publicly available at: https://github.com/solis-team/XRepoTest

</details>


### [35] [Vulnerable Code Search: Transferable Attack for Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26031)
*Kaicheng Wang,Liyan Huang,Jesse Thomason,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种编程语言无关、可迁移的对抗攻击方法，通过扰动代码片段中的标识符（不改变其功能）使无关代码与目标查询在嵌入空间中对齐，从而显著降低代码检索系统的检索性能。该攻击使用小型嵌入模型（如CodeT5+）即可生成，并能有效迁移到大型闭源模型（如Voyage-code-3）和LLM（如Gemini-3.1-Pro），可使SOTA模型的MRR最多下降77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的神经代码语言模型（CLMs）驱动的代码检索工具容易受到针对非功能性文本元素的对抗攻击，这种脆弱性可能导致检索结果被恶意操控，影响开发者生产力和代码复用效率。作者旨在揭示并利用这一漏洞。

Method: 提出一种编程语言无关、可迁移的对抗攻击方法。该方法扰动代码片段中的标识符（identifiers），在不改变代码功能的前提下，将代码的人工嵌入表示与目标查询对齐，从而"伪造"代码与查询之间的语义相似性。攻击在小型代码嵌入模型（如CodeT5+）上计算生成，验证其向大型闭源嵌入模型及LLM的迁移能力。

Result: 攻击可使查询与任意无关代码片段之间的相似度显著提升，使SOTA代码检索模型的MRR等关键检索指标最多下降77%。攻击在小型模型上计算即可有效迁移到更大的闭源模型。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了当前代码检索方法的脆弱性，强调了开发更鲁棒、更注重语义（而非表层文本特征）的代码检索方法的必要性。

Abstract: Reliable code retrieval is crucial for developer productivity and effective code reuse. However, current neural code language models (CLMs) powering search tools are susceptible to adversarial attacks targeting non-functional textual elements. In this paper, we introduce a programming language-agnostic, transferable, adversarial attack that exploits this CLM vulnerability. Our approach perturbs identifiers within a code snippet without altering the snippet's functionality to artificially align the code with a target query. We demonstrate that our attack, even when computed using smaller code embedding models, such as CodeT5+, is highly effective and transferable to larger, closed-source embedding models, like Voyage-code-3, or LLMs like Gemini-3.1-Pro. Our attack can increase the similarity between the query and arbitrary, irrelevant code snippets, consequently degrading key retrieval metrics such as the Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) of state-of-the-art models by up to 77%. The experimental results highlight the fragility of current code search methods and underscore the need for more robust, semantic-aware approaches.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 引入形式化框架：冻结底物D（不可变数据/代码快照）、接地族F（语义解释集合）、主张查询q及由此产生的识别集（identified set）。以此对固定提交（pinned commit）上所有124个机械上符合条件的Inspect Evals评估单元进行普查，对每个单元给出终止判定（terminal disposition），并区分执行闭合与中断情形，再从主张解析和primary/review族两个维度分离精确值、胜者、完整排序和两两关系。

Result: 全部124个单元均获得终止判定；其中110个在确定性推理之前就停止，原因是所需的历史证据或语义接地不可用。在能完成执行的单元中，精确值、胜者、完整顺序和两两关系在不同主张解析及primary与review族之间出现分歧。

Conclusion: 审计输出的是类型化的停止点、不稳定性证据和稳定子结构，而非强行赋予单一评估者含义或单一的健壮/不健壮标签，从而承认评估结果的多义性并刻画其稳定的核心内容。

Abstract: Evaluation artifacts specify a forward computation: a task, scorer, and reported metric. They do not necessarily license the claim attached to that metric because the historical evidence and alternative semantics needed to replay it may be unbound. We formalize this missing claim-replay layer through a frozen substrate D, a grounded family F, a claim query q, and the resulting identified set. We then census all 124 mechanically eligible Inspect Evals units at a pinned commit. Every unit receives a terminal disposition; 110 stop before deterministic inference because required historical evidence or semantic grounding is unavailable. Where execution closes, exact values, winners, complete orders, and pairwise relations separate by claim resolution and by primary versus review family. The audit therefore returns typed stops, instability witnesses, and stable substructure rather than forcing one evaluator meaning or one robust/not-robust label.

</details>

Abstract: Architectural floorplan images encode rich relational knowledge among functional spaces, which underpins design retrieval, knowledge-based reasoning, and BIM enrichment through the building lifecycle. However, it remains challenging to automatically construct task-adaptive graph representations for public buildings. To address this gap, we first define a multi-granularity Level-of-Graphs (LoGs) for public building layouts. Methodologically, we present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based automatic LoG construction through node identification, edge inference, text parsing, and graph coarsening. VLM-generated representations are systematically evaluated and tested in real-world tasks, using 147 academic library floorplans worldwide as a case study. Experiments showed VLM-generated graphs were broadly consistent with human-labeled graphs (matched node ratio >= 92%; 509.3 s per floor plan for three-LoG graph generation). Meso-grained graphs yield the best node-level zone prediction (Macro F1 = 0.647, at 65% of fine-grained complexity), while coarse-grained graphs are most effective for graph-level layout quality evaluation (Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.610, at 16% of fine-grained complexity). By enabling scalable, annotation-free extraction of structured layout information from floorplan images, this study advances design informatics by converting plan images into knowledge representations, thereby enhancing the utilization of design information across the building life cycle.

### [38] [SIMGUIDE: Procedurally Grounded Multi-Context Representations for Personalized Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24888)
*Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SIMGUIDE，一种将用户上下文结构化为类型化领域特定块（Sims）并用程序性示例锚定约束的方法，以解决个性化AI代理将用户视为单一扁平实体而无法处理跨生活情境冲突优先级的问题。基于47个SIMBENCH任务与100个τ-bench任务的评估表明，程序性Sims显著优于RAG基线（+7.9偏好遵循点），且参数级LoRA微调与按Sim类型路由适配器进一步提升了生成质量，揭示"表示格式而非表示内容"是首要设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化AI代理将用户表征为单一扁平配置文件的拼接，忽略了同一用户在不同生活情境中可能持有相互冲突的优先级。作者认为核心问题不在于代理缺乏用户信息，而在于用户表示的格式决定了代理能否利用该信息。这是对个性化系统设计范式的根本性质疑。

Method: SIMGUIDE方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 将用户上下文组织为类型化、领域特定的"Sims"块，每个约束配以源自过去决策的程序性示例；(2) SIMBENCH基准，包含47个偏好条件规划任务，正确规划取决于激活的用户上下文。此外，作者还在参数层面验证了相同原则，通过任务匹配的LoRA微调与按Sim类型而非用户身份路由适配器的方式进行实验。

Result: 声明式Sim约束单独并不优于RAG，但程序性Sims在GPT-4o上超越RAG达+7.9偏好遵循点（p=0.013），该优势在100个τ-bench任务上跨GPT-4o与Claude Sonnet 4.5复现（p≤0.023）。参数层面，任务匹配的LoRA较未适配基座提升12.8 ROUGE-L点，按Sim类型路由适配器额外增加7.3点，且对28%路由错误具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 用户表示的格式而非内容才是第一阶设计变量。上下文的结构化方式（类型化块+程序性示例）与参数适应的组织方式（按任务/Sim类型而非用户身份）对个性化代理性能起决定性作用，这一发现在推理（prompt）与参数（微调）两个层面上均得到验证。

Abstract: Personalized AI agents overwhelmingly treat users as single entities: a flat profile concatenated into a prompt. This fails when the same person holds different priorities across life contexts -- and fails catastrophically when those priorities conflict. The core problem is not that agents lack information about users; it is that the format of user representations determines whether an agent can act on that information at all. We introduce SIMGUIDE, a method that structures user context into typed, domain-specific blocks called Sims and grounds each constraint with procedural examples drawn from past decisions. To evaluate this, we construct SIMBENCH, a diagnostic suite of 47 preference-conditioned planning tasks where the correct plan depends on which user context is active -- a property no existing benchmark tests. Declarative Sim constraints alone do not outperform retrieval-based personalization (RAG). Procedurally grounded Sims outperform RAG on GPT-4o (+7.9 Preference Adherence points, $p = 0.013$), and this advantage replicates on 100 $τ$-bench tasks across both GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4.5 ($p \leq 0.023$). At the parametric level, the same principle holds: training distribution dominates whether parametric adaptation succeeds at all. Task-matched LoRA fine-tuning improves generation quality by 12.8 ROUGE-L points over the unadapted base model, and routing adapters by Sim type rather than user identity adds a further 7.3 points, robust to 28% routing error. Representation format -- not representation content -- is the first-order design variable.

</details>

*Nirmal Kumar Jingar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM驱动的决策引擎（LLM-DE），将大语言模型与数学优化、概率预测和安全约束决策过滤相结合，用于大规模供应链的端到端决策（需求预测、库存优化、运输路径规划和中断缓解），在保证安全性和可扩展性的前提下提升供应链决策的智能化水平。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则和纯优化的供应链系统难以应对高度不确定、动态且易中断的大规模供应链环境；同时，异构数据源（如交易需求信号和非结构化中断报告）的增多为构建能够同时推理、适应和优化的智能系统提供了机会。

Method: 提出一种混合架构LLM-DE，将LLM的语义推理能力与数学优化、概率预测以及安全约束决策过滤模块集成在一起，形成完整的端到端决策流水线，覆盖需求预测、库存优化、运输路由和中断缓解等环节。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLM语言推理与优化及形式化约束相结合，能够生成更智能、更安全且更具可扩展性的供应链决策，优于纯数据驱动或启发式方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种新型架构、完整算法流水线及数学模型化表述，为面向不确定性和大规模复杂性的下一代智能供应链基础设施奠定了务实的理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Current large-scale supply chains are highly uncertain, dynamic, and disruption prone that are challenging to serve up timely and resilient decisions through traditional rule-based and optimization-only systems. The increasing supply of heterogeneous data sources, such as transactional demand signals and unstructured disruption report, presents a chance of intelligent systems, which could reason, adapt and optimize at the same time. A hybrid architecture that combines large language models (LLMs) with mathematical optimization, probabilistic forecasting, and safety-constrained decision filtering is proposed in this paper as a performance of a Decision Engine, which is called LLM-Powered Decision Engine (LLM-DE). In comparison to purely data-driven or heuristic solutions, LLM-DE integrates semantic reasoning with LLM with a set of performance and safety guarantees that allow safe decision-making in large-scale supply chain processes. The suggested framework enables the end-to-end decision making such as demand forecasting, inventory optimization, and transportation routing and disruption mitigation. The findings affirm that language-based reasoning combined with optimization and formal constraints can be used to come up with not only smarter but also safer and more scalable supply chain decisions. This research provides a new architecture, a complete pipeline of algorithm, and a formulation based on mathematical constructs of the operational decision systems incorporating LLM. The proposed model offers a pragmatic and theoretical basis of the next-generation intelligent supply chain infrastructures that can be implemented to work dependably in the face of uncertainty and massive complexity.

</details>

Result: AI聊天机器人能够提升心理支持的可及性、缓解焦虑和抑郁症状并扩展社会心理支持，但仅有极少数系统针对非洲进行了本土化适配，多数仍根植于西方模式与英语设计，在本地相关性、包容性和可持续性方面存在关键缺口。

Conclusion: 研究提出了融合非洲文化价值观、多语言设计、移动优先优化与伦理保障的CADMH框架，并为AI聊天机器人在非洲医疗体系中的整合提供了研究、实践与政策层面的指导。

Abstract: Mental health challenges in Africa remain under-addressed due to inadequate infrastructure, stigma, and a chronic shortage of professionals. Artificial Intelligence (AI)-powered chatbots are emerging globally as low-cost, accessible tools that can offer psychological support. This paper presents a systematic review of 52 empirical studies published between 2017 and 2025, critically analysing their cultural, linguistic, and infrastructural relevance to African contexts. The findings demonstrate the potential of AI chatbots to improve accessibility, reduce symptoms of anxiety and depression, and expand psychosocial support, yet reveal limited African-specific adaptation. Most systems remain rooted in Western models and English-language designs, leaving critical gaps in local relevance, inclusivity, and sustainability. Based on the findings, the authors develop a Culturally Adaptive Digital Mental Health (CADMH) framework that integrates African cultural values, multilingual design, mobile-first optimisation, and ethical safeguards. The study highlights opportunities and barriers for integrating AI chatbots into African healthcare, offering guidance for research, practice, and policy.

</details>


### [41] [Measurement-Budget Allocation in Quantum Learning with Finite-Shot Generalization Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24891)
*Ferhat Ozgur Catak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了近端量子硬件上，在固定测量预算B下，如何权衡训练样本数n与每个样本的测量次数S，以优化二元量子分类器的泛化性能。作者证明了一个无分布泛化界，将有限样本项(√(d/n))与有限射击项(√((log n)/S))分离，并通过最小化保守代理函数得出最优分配规则n*=2√(2dB/log(2B/δ))，S*=B/n*，实现B^{-1/4}的渐近最坏情况速率，并通过PennyLane仿真验证了理论界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近端量子硬件上估计Born概率需要反复执行电路，量子学习实验在固定测量预算B下必须决定使用多少不同训练态n以及为每个态分配多少测量次数S。这一权衡(n与S的分配)此前缺乏严谨的理论指导，需要建立系统性的统计框架来指导预算分配。

Method: 作者针对固定或独立选择的测量算子M的二元量子分类器，证明了一个无分布泛化界，将有限样本贡献√(d/n)与有限射击贡献√((log n)/S)分离。在约束B=nS下，两项反向移动；通过最小化保守闭式代理函数，导出最优分配规则n*=2√(2dB/log(2B/δ))和S*=B/n*，并利用PennyLane对2量子比特和4量子比特变分量子电路在九个合成二元分类基准上进行仿真验证。

Result: 代理函数的渐近缩放与精确最小化器一致，实现B^{-1/4}的最坏情况速率。在所有测试配置中，单侧经验泛化间隙均低于理论界限，验证了理论保证的有效性。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了一个保守的统计指南，用于有限射击评估和预实验规划中测量预算的分配，补充了硬件级调度和电路设计方面的考虑。将保证推广到完全自适应的含噪射击训练仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: On near-term quantum hardware, estimating a Born probability requires repeated circuit executions. A quantum learning experiment with a fixed measurement budget $B$ must therefore decide how many distinct training states $n$ to use and how many shots $S$ to allocate to each state. We study this tradeoff for binary quantum classifiers with fixed or independently selected measurement operators $M$, where the ideal score is $\Tr(Mρ)$. We prove a distribution-free generalization bound that separates the finite-sample and finite-shot contributions. The sample term scales as $\sqrt{d/n}$, while the shot term scales as $\sqrt{(\log n)/S}$; under the constraint $B=nS$, these two terms move in opposite directions. Minimising a conservative closed-form surrogate of the bound gives the allocation rule $\nstar = 2\sqrt{2dB/\log(2B/δ)}$ and $\Sstar = B/\nstar$. This surrogate has the same asymptotic scaling as the exact minimizer and yields a worst-case rate of $B^{-1/4}$. The guarantee is intentionally conservative, since it applies to the full class of binary quantum measurements. We complement the theory with PennyLane simulations using 2-qubit and 4-qubit variational quantum circuits on nine synthetic binary classification benchmarks. In all tested configurations, the one-sided empirical generalization gap remains below the theoretical bound. The result provides a conservative statistical guideline for allocating measurement budgets in finite-shot evaluation and pre-experimental planning for near-term quantum learning systems, complementing hardware-level scheduling and circuit-design considerations. Extending the guarantee to fully adaptive shot-noisy training remains an open problem.

</details>


### [42] [Natural Language Input, Semantic Track Representation, and LLM Inference: Making the Maritime Information Exchange Model Tractable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24892)
*Frederick Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实用架构，利用大型语言模型（LLM）消除语义轨道模型（如海事信息交换模型MIEM和Rich Semantic Track模型）在国防与执法领域的采用障碍——不再要求操作员学习形式化本体语言，而是通过自然语言输入、LLM翻译为类型化语义断言记录（SAR）、知识图谱累积、第二轮LLM推理检测异常与假设排序的完整流水线，并以两个详细示例验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义轨道模型在国防和执法领域的采用受阻，因为操作员必须学习形式化本体语言并手动将观察结果编码为类型化逻辑断言。作者希望消除这一障碍，让操作员直接用自然语言贡献观察结果，从而推动模型在现有技术条件下的立即部署。

Method: 提出一个四阶段流水线：1）操作员以自然语言输入观察；2）LLM将自然语言翻译为类型化语义断言记录（SAR），即命名格框架（case frames），以紧凑结构捕获n元关系；3）知识图谱累积所有SAR；4）第二轮LLM对图执行推理、异常检测和假设排序。并通过9/11前兆指标场景和海事货物检查场景两个详细示例展示完整流程。

Result: 两个详细示例（9/11时代攻击前兆指标场景和海事货物检查场景）展示了从自然语言输入到SAR表示再到推理输出的完整pipeline，证明该架构在当前LLM技术下即可部署，并建立了对抗专有封闭方案的现有技术（prior art）。

Conclusion: 该架构使Track Model和MIEM在当前技术条件下即可部署，同时将语义轨道表示与神经流形几何联系起来，为方法提供理论框架支撑，并在防御与执法领域推动语义轨道模型的广泛采用。

Abstract: We describe a practical architecture for making the Maritime Information Exchange Model (MIEM) and the broader Rich Semantic Track model tractable using current large language model (LLM) technology. The barrier to adoption of semantic track models in defense and law enforcement has been the requirement that operators learn formal ontology languages and manually encode observations as typed logical assertions. We propose eliminating this barrier entirely: operators contribute observations in natural language; an LLM translates these into typed Semantic Assertion Records (SARs), which are named case frames that capture n-ary relations in a single compact structure; a knowledge graph accumulates the SARs; and a second LLM pass performs inference, anomaly detection, and hypothesis ranking over the graph. We work through two detailed examples (a 9/11-era pre-attack indicator scenario and a maritime cargo inspection scenario) showing the full pipeline from natural language input to SAR representation to inference output. We argue that this architecture makes the Track Model and MIEM immediately deployable with current technology, establishes prior art against proprietary enclosure of the approach, and grounds the method in a theoretical framework connecting semantic track representations to neural manifold geometry.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在竞争性FPS游戏（如CS2）中，账号完整性审查需要判断账号近期行为是否与历史操作者一致，涉及临时替补、段位代练、高分段玩家使用低段位账号等场景。人工审查需要将当前比赛与多场历史比赛逐一比较，效率低且主观性强，因此需要自动化的同玩家验证方法。

Method: 将审查任务形式化为同玩家验证问题：将单场比赛中单个玩家的行为轨迹编码为demo玩家行为指纹，涵盖准星控制、移动-停止-射击协调、经济/购买、战斗/交火、时间节奏五个维度。从1,330个CS2 demo中提取13,300个demo玩家观测，在8,840万候选对空间中采样663,590个同/异玩家对用于监督训练与评估。

Result: 最终配对模型平均ROC AUC达0.931，在95%精度下实现0.722的不同玩家召回率。特征分析表明，最强的身份信号来自低层操作（准星控制、射击节奏、移动-停止-射击协调），而非单场表现结果。在账号历史聚合评估中，历史深度从K=1的0.931 AUC提升至K=10的0.986。

Conclusion: CS2 demo行为数据能够支撑监督式同玩家验证和多demo历史聚合的账号级身份一致性建模，验证了低层机械习惯对玩家身份识别的有效性，为FPS游戏账号完整性审查提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: In competitive first-person shooter (FPS) games such as Counter-Strike 2 (CS2), account-integrity review often asks whether an account's recent behavior remains consistent with its historical operator. This consistency question arises in cases such as temporary substitution, rank boosting, and high-skill players using lower-ranked accounts, where manual review requires comparing a current match against multiple historical matches. We formulate this review task as same-player verification: we encode the behavioral trajectory of a single player in a match replay (demo) as a demo-player behavioral fingerprint, and train a model to judge whether two behavioral observations come from the same real player. Grounded in CS2 game understanding, the fingerprints cover crosshair control, movement-stop-fire coordination, economy/buy, combat/engagement, and temporal rhythm. From 1,330 CS2 demos we extract 13,300 demo-player observations, and sample 663,590 same/different pairs from an 88.4M candidate-pair space for supervised training and evaluation. The final pairwise model reaches an average ROC AUC of 0.931 and achieves 0.722 different-player recall at 95% precision. Feature analysis shows that the strongest identity signals come from low-level operations, especially crosshair control, firing rhythm, and movement-stop-fire coordination, indicating that stable low-level mechanical habits are more informative for this verification task than single-match performance outcomes. In the account-history aggregation evaluation, increasing history depth raises AUC from the K=1 single-pair baseline of 0.931 to 0.986 at K=10. These results show that CS2 demo behavior can support supervised same-player verification and account-level identity-consistency modeling through multi-demo history aggregation.

</details>

*Grama Chethan*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于制造企业工业数字线程语义图统一的开源框架。该框架通过本体驱动的RDF知识图谱,融合了11个模拟来源、九大领域的数据,采用五阶段ETL流水线和自动化实体解析(97条owl:sameAs链接),包含78个RDFS类、108个对象属性和243个数据属性。关键实证结果表明:阻断24个跨系统工具会使召回率从1.00降至0.31(F1从1.00降至0.48),证明69%的可发现信号需要跨系统图连接;消融实验确认九个策略中六个贡献了独特信号;65信号清单验证得到F1=1.00。图通过287个Model Context Protocol工具以SPARQL原生语义层暴露给LLM代理,并为五个制造垂直领域提供行业模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造企业存在ERP、MES、PLM、SCADA、QMS、SCM等异构系统,每个系统都有独立的数据模型和API,形成数据孤岛,阻碍整体分析、延迟根本原因调查,并妨碍工业4.0的可追溯性。点对点集成复杂度呈O(n²)增长且积累脆弱依赖,亟需一种统一的语义框架来融合跨系统数据。

Method: 提出一个本体驱动的语义图统一框架:①构建RDF知识图谱,参考ISA-95、OPC UA、eClass、Asset Administration Shell、RAMI 4.0等标准;②设计五阶段ETL流水线,融合11个模拟来源的数据,涵盖九大领域;③实现自动化实体解析,生成97条owl:sameAs身份链接;④开发自动化发现引擎,应用九种策略类别(跨站关联、告警覆盖、ECN影响、CUSUM/EWMA漂移检测等);⑤通过287个Model Context Protocol工具以SPARQL原生语义层将图暴露给LLM代理;⑥为五个制造垂直行业(航空航天、消费品、制药、医疗器械、涡轮叶片)提供行业模板。

Result: 主要实证结果:①阻断24个跨系统工具使召回率从1.00降至0.31,F1从1.00降至0.48,表明69%的可发现信号需要跨系统图连接;②留一法消融实验确认九个策略中六个贡献了独特信号;③对65信号清单(16个阳性、49个空值)验证得到F1=1.00,95% Clopper-Pearson置信区间[0.79, 1.00];④五个行业模板展示了跨制造垂直领域的模式稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了语义图方法对工业数字线程统一的可行性,证明跨系统集成对于信号发现至关重要。但作者明确指出,由于验证清单为作者自行构建,该结果属于验证(verification)而非独立验证(validation)。框架通过MCP工具为LLM代理提供语义层,展示了将图论、本体和AI代理结合用于制造业数据统一的潜力。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing enterprises operate heterogeneous systems -- ERP, MES, PLM, SCADA, QMS, SCM -- each with its own data model and API. The resulting silos prevent holistic analysis, delay root-cause investigation, and obstruct Industry 4.0 traceability. Point-to-point integration scales as O(n^2) and accumulates brittle dependencies. This paper presents an open framework for semantic graph unification of industrial digital threads. An ontology-driven RDF knowledge graph unifies data from 11 simulated sources across nine domains through a five-stage ETL pipeline with automated entity resolution spanning 97 owl:sameAs identity links. The ontology encompasses 78 RDFS classes, 108 object properties, and 243 data properties, drawing on ISA-95, OPC UA, eClass, the Asset Administration Shell, RAMI 4.0, and additional standards. An automated discovery engine applies nine strategy categories -- cross-station correlation, alarm coverage, ECN impact, CUSUM/EWMA drift detection -- to surface insights spanning system boundaries. The primary empirical result: blocking 24 cross-system tools reduces recall from 1.00 to 0.31 (F1 from 1.00 to 0.48), showing that 69% of discoverable signals require cross-system graph joins. Leave-one-out ablation confirms six of nine strategies contribute unique signals. Verification against a 65-signal manifest (16 positive, 49 null) yields F1 = 1.00 (95% Clopper-Pearson CI [0.79, 1.00]); as the manifest was author-constructed, this constitutes verification not independent validation. The graph is exposed to LLM agents via 287 Model Context Protocol tools as a SPARQL-native semantic layer. Five industry templates (aerospace, CPG, pharma, medical devices, turbine blades) demonstrate schema stability across manufacturing verticals.

</details>


### [45] [post-graph-rag: A PostgreSQL-Native Graph RAG Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24921)
*Chandan Rajah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: post-graph-rag 是一个开源检索增强生成引擎，将文本块向量、规范实体图和社区摘要统一存储在一个 PostgreSQL 数据库中，通过提取期不变量、谓词规范化、实体消歧和时序层解决传统 GraphRAG 在基础设施、图质量和时间演化三方面的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于图的检索增强生成（GraphRAG）需要同时维护向量库、图数据库和文档库三套基础设施，且提取流程从不拒绝输出导致图中填充了无意义的关系边；此外图只增不减，无法区分过期和当前事实。这些问题造成了基础设施冗余、图质量低和时间演化缺失三大痛点。

Method: 将所有组件（文本块、嵌入、规范实体图、社区摘要）统一放入单一 PostgreSQL 数据库，使用 pgvector 做向量搜索、边表做图遍历。在写入之前执行提取期不变量：拒绝模糊谓词、代词名称和裸数量；将谓词规范化到可选词汇表；通过模型提供的别名将实体解析为每个规范名称的单一顶点；对否定关系使用否定标志保留正向谓词。时序层允许关系携带来自文本的有效期，允许后文文档通过文档顺序覆盖早期冲突断言，并支持按时间点（as-of）查询。

Result: 在三个语料库上使用相同的提取和嵌入模型与 LightRAG 对比，post-graph-rag 在所有数据集上构建了更密集的图，每实体关系数最多达 LightRAG 的 2.4 倍；查询性更好，每关系的不同边标签在 0.46 到 0.58（受控词汇表下为 0.11），而 LightRAG 为 0.77 到 1.33；查询延迟更低且回答质量相当；支持基线不具备的时序演化，在时间序列和十年备案数据上分别有 13 和 8 条关系被取代，而基线为零。

Conclusion: post-graph-rag 通过单一数据库架构消除了多存储基础设施的一致性负担，通过提取期不变量显著提升图质量和可查询性，并通过时序层实现了事实的时间演化能力。作者强调这些是工程测量而非基准测试结果。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation connects facts that no single passage states, but current implementations pay for that three times: in infrastructure, requiring a vector store, graph database and document store to be kept consistent; in graph quality, because an extraction pipeline that never refuses output fills the graph with edges that assert nothing; and over time, because a graph that only accumulates treats superseded and current facts alike.
  post-graph-rag is an open-source engine addressing all three. Text chunks with embeddings, a canonical entity graph and community summaries live in one PostgreSQL database, with pgvector for search and edge tables for traversal. Extraction-time invariants run before anything is written: vague predicates, pronominal names and bare quantities are rejected; predicates are normalised onto an optional vocabulary; entities resolve to one vertex per canonical name via model-supplied aliases; and denied relations keep the positive predicate under a negation flag. A temporal layer lets relations carry a validity period from the prose, lets a later document supersede an earlier incompatible assertion from document order alone, and answers as-of queries.
  Against LightRAG on three corpora under identical extraction and embedding models, post-graph-rag builds a denser graph everywhere, up to $2.4\times$ the relations per entity, and a more queryable one: distinct edge labels run at 0.46 to 0.58 per relation, 0.11 under a controlled vocabulary, against 0.77 to 1.33. It answers comparably with lower query latency, and supports temporal evolution the baseline lacks: 13 and 8 relationships superseded on a novel sequence and a decade of filings, against zero. These are engineering measurements, not a benchmark result.
  Code: https://github.com/crajah/post-graph-rag, https://github.com/crajah/post-graph

</details>


### [46] [FrontierChallenge: Evaluating Scientific Workflow Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24979)
*Liangcai Su,Zhaopeng Feng,Zhuo Chen,Zhen Zhang,Xiang Lin,Ruilin Li,Handuo Zhang,Ning Wang,Kailong Wen,Yueqi Guo,Feng Xing,Yiling Guo,Chenxiong Qian,Simon Shaolei Du,Lidong Bing,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FrontierChallenge，一个包含300个端到端科学工作流的跨领域基准（当前发布并评估其中97个任务），覆盖量子化学、分子动力学、材料表征、分析化学、生命科学、电化学/环境等领域。对12个前沿模型配合3种智能体脚手架进行评估，结果显示最佳配置在97个任务中仅完成20个（通过率20.6%）。分析化学和电化学/环境领域的平均得分分别达87.6和94.9，但最高通过率仅4%和0%。此外，75.5%未通过的Claude Code轨迹中语言声称已完成但实际未完成，表明高分和自信的完成声明都无法可靠反映科学任务的完整交付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体基准大多聚焦于最终答案、孤立程序或单一领域，缺乏对跨领域端到端科学工作流完成度的系统评估，无法真实反映科学智能体在实际研究任务中的交付能力。作者旨在填补这一空白，评估前沿模型在工作流集成执行和科学交付物完整性上的实际表现。

Method: 构建FrontierChallenge基准，包含300个端到端科学工作流任务（当前发布97个），覆盖六个科学领域。每个任务提供固定输入并规定一组必须交付的科学产物。采用两种评估指标：Pass Rate（完整满足全部门槛的任务比例）和Avg. Score（反映部分进展）。对12个前沿模型使用3种智能体脚手架（含Claude Code）进行系统评估。

Result: 最佳配置仅完成20/97个任务，Pass Rate为20.6%。分析化学和电化学/环境领域的Avg. Score分别达87.6和94.9，但最高Pass Rate仅为4%和0%，说明部分进展到完整交付的转化极差。在未通过的Claude Code轨迹中，75.5%以声称完成的语言结束。

Conclusion: 高部分得分和自信的完成声明都不能可靠地指示科学任务已被完整交付。科学智能体的评估必须同时关注端到端工作流执行和科学交付物的完整性，该基准揭示了当前前沿模型在复杂科学任务完整交付上的显著不足。

Abstract: Scientific agents increasingly analyze data, execute code, and produce research artifacts, yet most benchmarks emphasize final answers, isolated programs, or a single domain. We introduce FrontierChallenge, a cross-domain benchmark comprising 300 end-to-end scientific workflows. In this paper, we release and evaluate 97 of these tasks, spanning quantum chemistry, molecular dynamics, materials characterization, analytical chemistry, life science, and electrochemistry/environment. Each task provides fixed inputs and specifies a bundle of required scientific deliverables. We evaluate twelve frontier models with three agent scaffolds. Pass Rate measures the fraction of tasks satisfying the full-completion criterion, while Avg. Score captures partial progress. Each of the best-performing configurations completed only 20 of the 97 released tasks, yielding a Pass Rate of 20.6%. Partial progress translated especially poorly into complete delivery in analytical chemistry and electrochemistry/environment: Avg. Scores reached 87.6 and 94.9, but the highest Pass Rates were only 4% and 0%. Among non-passing Claude Code trajectories, 75.5% still ended with language claiming completion. These findings show that neither high partial scores nor confident claims of completion reliably indicate that a scientific task has been fully delivered, highlighting the need to evaluate end-to-end workflow execution and the completeness of scientific deliverables together.

</details>


### [47] [Solving Robust POMDPs with Omega-regular Objectives via Partially Observable Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24986)
*Durgam Latha,Dion Reji,S. Akshay,Djordje Zikelic,Shankaranarayanan Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了鲁棒部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（RPOMDPs）在一般ω-正则目标下的求解问题，首次建立了(s,a)-矩形RPOMDPs（多面体不确定性集）与部分可观测随机博弈（POSGs）之间的双向等价归约关系，并由此推导出一系列新的计算复杂度上下界结果，同时给出了RMDPs的新复杂度结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统POMDP假设转移概率精确已知，但在实际应用中转移概率往往仅知道属于某个不确定性集合。RPOMDPs推广了POMDP以处理这种不确定性，但其在ω-正则目标下的求解复杂度尚未被充分理解，需要建立其与已有模型（如POSGs）之间的关系以获得复杂度刻画。

Method: 作者证明，(s,a)-矩形RPOMDPs（多面体不确定性集）在ω-正则目标下的求解问题可以归约为POSGs在ω-正则目标下的求解问题；并进一步首次证明反向归约也成立，从而建立了两者之间的双向归约与语义等价关系。基于这一等价性，推导出求解不同ω-正则目标下RPOMDPs的计算复杂度上界和下界。

Result: 首次建立了(s,a)-矩形RPOMDPs（多面体不确定性集）与POSGs之间的双向归约和语义等价性；由此获得了一系列关于RPOMDPs在不同ω-正则目标（涵盖可达性、安全性、LTL目标等）下的新的计算复杂度上界与下界结果；作为推论，还得到了RMDPs的新计算复杂度结果。

Conclusion: 该工作通过揭示RPOMDPs与POSGs之间的深层语义等价关系，为理解RPOMDPs的计算复杂性提供了统一框架，并为RMDPs的复杂度分析带来了新的推论，推动了鲁棒规划与决策理论的发展。

Abstract: Robust POMDPs (RPOMDPs) generalize classical POMDPs to the setting where exact transition probabilities are not known -- rather, they are only known to belong to some uncertainty set of values. In this work, we study the problem of solving RPOMDPs with general omega-regular objectives, which subsume a broad class of objectives such as reachability, safety, and linear temporal logic (LTL) objectives. We show that, for (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets, the problem of solving RPOMDPs under omega-regular objectives can be reduced to solving partially observable stochastic games (POSGs) under omega-regular objectives. Moreover, we show for the first time that reductions can be constructed in both directions, establishing the semantic equivalence between (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets and POSGs. This allows us to derive a range of new computational complexity results, including both upper and lower complexity bounds, on solving RPOMDPs with different omega-regular objectives. As a corollary, we also derive new computational complexity results for RMDPs.

</details>


### [48] [CVE-SAI: Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing for Risk-Controlled E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25023)
*Xiaolong Sun,Qichao Wang,Hangyu Li,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CVE-SAI（反事实视觉证据引导的选择性属性索引）框架，将多模态产品模型的属性推断与索引准入解耦，通过反事实视觉干预构建属性相关的视觉依赖代理，在冻结候选答案后再执行证据审计，从而在风险预算约束下实现视觉可支持的属性补全与安全索引准入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态产品模型在补全缺失电商属性时，仅优化属性-答案准确率而不验证视觉支持；将瞬态预测与持久性索引准入混为一谈；且缺乏对事实错误或视觉不支持值的显式风险控制。这些缺口会导致不准确的候选进入持久索引，造成索引污染。

Method: CVE-SAI由两部分核心组成：Focus-Zone Distortion (FZD)通过受控反事实干预构造属性特定的视觉依赖代理；Evidence-Guided Attention Redistribution (EGAR)利用该代理细化受本体约束的评分。关键步骤是先冻结本体约束的候选答案（仅从主图像和属性问题推断，不参考目录文本），再依次执行证据必要性、证据保留、干扰变换稳定性、候选特定目录文本冲突等审计；目录文本只能收紧准入而不能修改候选。最后通过独立家族级校准在5%不安全准入预算下选择单一策略，并给出同时一侧有限样本界。

Result: 在Amazon Berkeley Objects上五个视觉属性的实验中，CVE-SAI在属性推断和证据定位上均有提升；在共享风险协议下取得最高的认证准入覆盖率；在自动准入系统中取得最强的受控检索性能及最低的不安全自动诱导暴露。

Conclusion: 将推断与准入分离可使视觉支持的属性补全改善检索性能，同时限制持久索引污染，表明推断与准入的解耦是多模态属性索引中风险控制的关键设计原则。

Abstract: Multimodal product models can complete missing e-commerce attributes, yet current methods still optimize attribute-answer accuracy without verifying visual support, conflate transient prediction with persistent index admission, and lack explicit risk control over factually incorrect or visually unsupported values. We address these gaps with Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing (CVE-SAI), which first infers and freezes an ontology-constrained candidate from the primary image and attribute question without catalog text, and then decides whether that candidate should enter the index. Focus-Zone Distortion (FZD) constructs an attribute-specific visual-dependence proxy through a controlled counterfactual intervention, and Evidence-Guided Attention Redistribution (EGAR) uses the proxy to refine ontology-constrained scoring. The canonical candidate is frozen before evidence necessity, evidence retention, nuisance-transformation stability, and candidate-specific catalog-text conflict audits; catalog text can only tighten admission and cannot revise the candidate. Independent family-level calibration selects one policy with a simultaneous one-sided finite-sample bound under a 5% unsafe-admission budget. Experiments on five visual attributes derived from Amazon Berkeley Objects show that CVE-SAI improves attribute inference and evidence localization, achieves the highest certified admission coverage under the shared risk protocol, and yields the strongest controlled retrieval performance with the lowest unsafe auto-induced exposure among automatic-admission systems. Separating inference from admission therefore enables visually supported attribute completion to improve retrieval while limiting persistent index contamination.

</details>


### [49] [Retrieve, Match, Escalate: Accurate and Scalable Product Linking with VLM-Distilled Cross-Encoders and Agentic VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25037)
*Jian Wang,Steven Xu,Sanjyot Thete,Maryam Barouti,Tom Tang,Elaine Wu,Charu Sareen,Kyle MacDonald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个生产级的"检索-匹配"级联系统用于产品链接（product linking）任务。该系统根据难度分配计算资源：检索层找出候选匹配，轻量文本交叉编码器自动解决高置信度的多数案例，而多模态视觉-语言智能体通过检查产品图片和发起网络搜索来解决模糊的剩余案例。交叉编码器从数百万双VLM共识标签中蒸馏而来，消除了人工标注，并校准到98%精度。自托管的开源智能体以约七分之一的单对成本，在未微调的情况下达到闭源前沿VLM的精度（牺牲4个百分点的召回率）。级联将端到端链接覆盖率从68%提升到77%，单对成本横跨近五个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在产品链接任务中，海量多品类记录需要匹配到数千万规范产品。一个单一模型要么对困难案例不够强，要么对简单案例过于昂贵。需要在生产级规模下实现"按难度分配计算"的高性价比解决方案。

Method: 提出一个三阶段检索-匹配级联：1) 检索层获取候选匹配；2) 轻量文本交叉编码器自动解决高置信度多数案例（从数百万双VLM共识标签中蒸馏，校准至98%精度自动接受线，无需人工标注）；3) 自托管开源多模态VLM智能体处理模糊剩余案例，通过图片检查和网络搜索获取证据，无需微调即可达到闭源前沿VLM精度。

Result: 自托管开源智能体在未微调情况下以闭源VLM四分之一左右的单对成本达到其精度，但召回率低4个点（88%对92%）。级联通过仅将困难尾部升级到智能体，将端到端覆盖率从廉价阶段68%提升到77%。单对成本从廉价交叉编码器到前沿VLM跨越近五个数量级。

Conclusion: 级联架构通过按难度分配计算资源，在生产级产品链接中实现了成本-覆盖率的最优平衡。蒸馏自VLM共识的交叉编码器取代了人工标注，自托管智能体在成本敏感的生产环境中是闭源前沿VLM的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Product linking, the entity-resolution task of mapping merchant product records to canonical catalog products, consolidates fragmented listings so downstream search, recommendation, and advertising see one clean entry per product. At marketplace scale, billions of noisy, multi-category records must be resolved against tens of millions of canonical products, where scoring every candidate with a single model is either too weak for the hard cases or too costly for the easy ones. We present a production retrieve-then-match cascade that spends computation in proportion to difficulty: retrieval surfaces plausible matches, a lightweight text cross-encoder auto-resolves the high-confidence majority, and an agentic multimodal vision-language model settles the ambiguous remainder by inspecting product images and issuing web searches for evidence that is in neither record. The cross-encoder is distilled from millions of dual-VLM-consensus labels, retiring human annotation from the training set, and is calibrated to auto-accept links at a 98% precision bar validated against a smaller operator-certified audit. The agent is a self-hosted open-weight model that reaches a closed frontier VLM's precision at a four-point recall cost (88% versus 92%) for roughly one-seventh the per-pair cost, with no fine-tuning. Per-pair cost spans nearly five orders of magnitude from the cheap cross-encoder to the frontier VLM, so escalating only the hard tail to the agent raises end-to-end link coverage from the cheap stage's 68% to 77%.

</details>


### [50] [LifePlanner: Evaluating LLM Agents for Geo-spatial Planning with Social Media Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25039)
*Zhen Dong,Yuning Peng,Yutao Shi,Lei Zhong,Yongsen Mao,Yuan Liu,Haiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LifePlanner是一个新基准测试，将地图数据与大规模本地社交媒体帖子结合，通过MCP工具集提供访问，用于评估LLM代理在地理空间规划（如行程设计）中的表现。实验表明，前沿LLM在简单检索任务上表现良好，但在复杂规划任务上性能急剧下降（Pass Rate降至40.2%），失败主要源于不完整的证据获取、不精确的工具使用和薄弱的约束整合，而非模型规模或推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间规划基准仅提供干净的地图数据和工具，缺乏人们在日常规划中依赖的开放式社交信号（如本地社交媒体帖子）。作者希望创建一个更真实的测试环境，以评估LLM代理在处理嘈杂证据、多约束推理和接地工具使用方面的能力。

Method: LifePlanner基准通过将地图数据与大规模本地社交媒体帖子结合来丰富数据，并通过MCP（Model Context Protocol）工具集提供访问。该基准包含四个任务类别和三个难度级别的评估套件。

Result: 前沿LLM在简单检索任务上表现良好，但在复杂规划任务上性能急剧下降，Pass Rate降至40.2%。失败主要源于不完整的证据获取（来自大规模多模态数据库）、不精确的工具使用和薄弱的约束整合，而非模型大小或推理长度。

Conclusion: 未来进展需要有效的接地规划（grounded planning）而非单纯扩展模型规模，这为LLM代理在地理空间规划领域的改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Geo-spatial planning, like trip design, is a realistic testbed for LLM agents because it requires grounded tool use, noisy evidence retrieval, and multi-constraint reasoning. Most benchmarks, however, only provide clean geospatial data and tools, missing the open-ended social signals that people use in daily planning. We introduce LifePlanner, a benchmark that enriches map data with large-scale local social media posts and provides access through an MCP toolset. LifePlanner provides an evaluation suite spanning four task categories and three difficulty levels. Experiments show frontier LLMs perform well on simple retrieval but degrade sharply on complex planning, with the Pass Rate dropping to 40.2%. Results show that failures mainly stem from incomplete evidence acquisition from such a large multimodal database, imprecise tool use, and weak constraint integration rather than model size or reasoning length, suggesting that future progress requires effective grounded planning instead of scaling alone.

</details>


### [51] [SimVerity: When Does Simulated Agent Success Survive Physical Deployment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25067)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Yefan Zhang,Krinos Li,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SimVerity框架，系统量化模拟评估结果向物理部署迁移的可靠性。通过在真实智能家居部署中重放匹配场景并与物理见证者交叉验证，发现模拟中的"通过"并不可靠：先进模拟器通过全部240个灯光试验，但摄像头捕获了42次亚秒级失败。SimVerity将判定转移转化为明确的决策：清除、弃权或在部署前升级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理的模拟评估被广泛使用，但模拟通过结果能为物理部署提供多少证据尚未被系统量化。现有工作缺乏对模拟到真实环境迁移可靠性的形式化度量，导致虚假通过（false clearance）问题难以被识别和防范。

Method: SimVerity采用判定转移保证框架：(1) 在目标智能家居部署中重放匹配场景；(2) 与独立合格物理见证者交叉验证代理执行；(3) 学习风险画像并在评估前锁定；(4) 测量代理可审计性（切换代理模型客户端/服务配置观察场景匹配率变化）；(5) 引入第二合格模拟器检验独立交叉验证能力。

Result: 先进模拟器通过全部240个灯光试验，但物理相机捕获42次亚秒级失败；风险画像预测在11个保留会话中全部击败属性盲基线；切换代理配置使场景匹配率从52-88%提升至100%；第二模拟器从未在任何重叠案例中提出异议，仅物理测量揭示了共同盲点。

Conclusion: 部署成功是真实世界过程而非模拟中的静态属性，完成状态、已报告状态、可观察效果和最终结果可能在同一执行中分歧。模拟器之间无法提供独立交叉验证，只有物理测量能暴露共享盲点。SimVerity将判定转移从隐式假设转变为明确的清除/弃权/升级决策流程。

Abstract: Simulated evaluation is widely used to benchmark AI agents, yet how much evidence a simulated pass provides about physical deployment has not been systematically quantified.
