Method: 通过大规模经验探查（extensive empirical probing），在不同架构和超参数下考察FedAvg和SCAFFOLD的优化动态，测量锐度（sharpness）与学习率、数据异构程度、本地步数之间的关系，并分析SCAFFOLD的全局梯度估计误差与锐度的相关性。

Result: 1) FedAvg和SCAFFOLD在多种架构和超参数下均出现EoS-like动态；2) 锐度的平衡值反比于学习率，且数据异构程度影响该平衡值（本地步数则不产生影响）；3) 在EoS状态下，SCAFFOLD的全局梯度估计能力严重退化，其估计误差与锐度呈显著相关性。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD在深度学习实践中表现不佳的可能机制是：在EoS处高锐度状态下，SCAFFOLD无法可靠地估计全局梯度，这解释了其理论与实践性能之间的差距。

Abstract: In federated learning, it is well known that heterogeneous data can (in theory) slow down optimization, and much effort has been directed at designing optimization algorithms that are unaffected by data heterogeneity, such as the SCAFFOLD algorithm. Yet, despite strong theoretical guarantees, SCAFFOLD does not usually outperform the much simpler FedAvg in practice. In this work, we propose that this gap is due to the presence of Edge of Stability (EoS) and progressive sharpening in federated optimization, supported by extensive empirical probing. First, we find that EoS-like dynamics occur with both FedAvg and SCAFFOLD under a variety of architectures and hyperparameters. We observe that the equilibrium value of the sharpness is inversely proportional to the learning rate (as in GD), and interestingly, the degree of data heterogeneity (but not the number of local steps) also affects the equilibrium value. Most importantly, we observe that SCAFFOLD's ability to estimate the gradient of the global objective is severely degraded at the EoS, as measured by the correlation between sharpness and SCAFFOLD's error in estimating the global gradient along the optimization trajectory. This suggests a mechanism for SCAFFOLD's lackluster performance in deep learning: with high sharpness at the EoS, SCAFFOLD cannot reliably estimate the global gradient.

</details>


### [183] [A General-Purpose Molecular Foundation Model Transfers Across Diverse Olfactory Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25893)
*Yikun Han,Yi Wang,Neil Mankodi,Stephen Yang,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将分子基础模型Uni-Mol2在单一气味描述符预测任务（GS-LF基准）上微调，证明其学习到的表征无需额外深度学习训练即可迁移到四个互补的下游任务（跨数据集气味描述符预测、有气味vs无气味分类、对映体评估、气味混合物可辨别性），支持"一次训练、跨任务迁移"的机器嗅觉范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型已变革分子性质预测，但尚不清楚在单一典型嗅觉预测任务上微调的分子基础模型能否学到可迁移到多种不同机器嗅觉问题的表征。作者旨在探究这一核心问题。

Method: 在GS-LF多标签气味描述符预测基准上微调Uni-Mol2，然后在四个互补下游场景中进行零训练（不额外进行深度学习训练）评估：跨数据集气味描述符预测、有气味与无气味分类、对映体评估及气味混合物可辨别性。

Result: 微调后的模型在GS-LF主基准上达到或超越气味领域特定最先进基线，并在所有下游评估中持续实现迁移。对映体分析进一步表明三维分子表征能够区分镜像分子，而二维图模型在根本上无法做到；但准确预测立体化学的感知后果仍是开放挑战。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持机器嗅觉的"一次训练、跨任务迁移"范式，并表明化学预训练的分子表征为可迁移的嗅觉预测提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Foundation models have transformed molecular property prediction, yet it remains unclear whether a molecular foundation model, fine-tuned on a single canonical olfactory prediction task, can learn representations that transfer across diverse machine olfaction problems. We investigate this question by fine-tuning Uni-Mol2 on the GS-LF benchmark for multi-label odor descriptor prediction and evaluating the resulting model, without additional deep-learning training, on four complementary downstream settings: cross-dataset odor descriptor prediction, odorous-versus-odorless classification, enantiomer evaluation, and odor mixture discriminability. The fine-tuned model matches or exceeds the performance of the state-of-the-art olfaction-specific baseline on the primary GS-LF benchmark and consistently transfers across these downstream evaluations. The enantiomer analysis further shows that three-dimensional molecular representations distinguish mirror-image molecules in a way that two-dimensional graph models fundamentally cannot, although accurately predicting the perceptual consequences of stereochemistry remains an open challenge. Together, these results support a train-once, transfer-across-tasks paradigm for machine olfaction and suggest that chemically pretrained molecular representations provide a strong foundation for transferable olfactory prediction.

</details>


### [184] [Towards A Unified Information Bottleneck Framework for Time Series Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25897)
*Xu Zheng,Zichuan Liu,Zhuomin Chen,Mayur Akewar,Janki Bhimani,Jason Liu,Mo Sha,Jingchao Ni,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的时间序列可解释性框架（\modelname），将归因解释与反事实解释整合到同一信息论目标函数中，基于信息瓶颈原理防止平凡解和分布偏移，生成忠实的归因和稳定的反事实解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列解释方法分为归因解释和反事实解释两大类别，但两者被孤立研究：归因方法缺乏因果验证，反事实方法存在严重的不稳定性，容易产生类似对抗扰动的噪声而非有意义的解释。作者从信息论角度重新审视时间序列可解释性，指出现有解释器容易受到平凡解和分布偏移的影响。

Method: 提出一个统一的目标函数，将归因推理与反事实推理结合在单一框架中。该目标函数基于信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）原理，显式阻止平凡解释和分布外反事实的产生。在此目标函数基础上，引入\modelname框架，通过学习一个参数化变换网络来构造嵌入解释的实例：保留的信息产生归因解释，受控的信息移除产生稳定的反事实解释。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界基准上，\modelname与最先进的基线方法进行了广泛评估。定量和定性结果表明，\modelname在忠实性归因和稳定性反事实解释方面均持续优于对比方法。

Conclusion: \modelname通过统一的框架有效连接了归因与反事实解释两大领域，既为归因提供了因果层面的验证，又解决了反事实方法的不稳定性问题，在合成与真实基准上均取得了优越的性能。

Abstract: Explaining deep learning models operating on time series data is crucial in various applications that require transparent and interpretable insights into model behavior. {Existing explanation methods generally fall into two categories: attribution-based explanations, which identify the temporal regions most responsible for a prediction, and counterfactual explanations, which reveal how an input should be modified to alter the model's decision.} {Despite valuable insights, these two fields are largely studied independently. This disconnect leaves attribution methods lacking causal validation, while counterfactual methods suffer from severe instability, producing adversarial-like noise instead of meaningful explanations.} In this work, we revisit time-series explainability from an information-theoretic perspective and show that existing explainers are vulnerable to trivial solutions and distributional shifts. To address these limitations, we propose a unified objective function for explainable time series learning that bridges attribution and counterfactual reasoning within a single framework. Building upon the Information Bottleneck principle, our formulation explicitly prevents trivial explanations and out-of-distribution counterfactuals. {Based on this objective function, we introduce {\modelname}, a novel explanation framework that learns a parametric transformation network to construct explanation-embedded instances, where preserved information yields attribution explanations and controlled information removal produces stable counterfactual explanations.} We evaluate {\modelname} on synthetic and real-world benchmarks against state-of-the-art baselines. Extensive quantitative and qualitative results show that {\modelname} consistently outperforms competing methods, yielding faithful attributions and stable counterfactual explanations.

</details>


### [185] [Forecasting Multiple Observables with SCROLL: Score-Trained Uncertainty for Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25898)
*Pavel Prochazka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的多任务随机动力系统预测方法：在共享骨干网络上，为每个观测任务（未来状态、阈值事件、状态标签）构建自由路由的最后一层"信念"（likelihood），通过同一次梯度反传学习似然参数，从而自动吸收不同任务的单位尺度差异。在Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程上验证了方法在良设定、同方差情形下能恢复解析核并与正确基线持平；在异方差系统（随机Lorenz-63、真实空气质量数据）上，输入依赖的方差使方法在状态与状态标签任务上取得最优单次运行NLL，且达到与更优NLL对抗方法相当或更好的校准效果，计算成本远低于调参网格搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机动力系统的预测往往不是单一数值，而是需要同时预测多个可观测目标（未来状态、阈值事件、状态标签等），每个都有各自的似然。标准多任务方法是按任务加权损失、手动或学习调节权重。本文动机在于：利用随机动力学本身可计算预测方差这一特性，设计一种无需显式平衡任务损失、能自动处理单元相关尺度差异的替代方案。

Method: 方法是在共享骨干网络上，为每个可观测任务使用"自由路由"的最后一层信念（即每个任务各自独立的似然层），并利用似然参数在同一梯度反传中学习，从而把每个任务依赖的尺度缩放吸收进似然参数中。相比静态基准，随机动力学提供可解析计算的真实预测方差作为训练/验证标准。

Result: 在良设定、同方差的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程上，学习到的预测法则恢复了解析核，且与正确指定的基线持平。在异方差系统（随机Lorenz-63、真实空气质量数据）上，信念的输入依赖方差带来分离效果：在状态与状态标签任务上取得最优单次运行NLL，校准效果与NLL更优的对抗方法相当，但仅在调参网格成本的一小部分内实现。在真实序列上，状态边缘在五个滚动起点上保持优势。

Conclusion: 所提的逐任务自由路由最后一层信念方法能够在随机动力系统多任务预测中，无需显式损失加权即可实现最优或相当的预测似然与校准效果，且计算成本显著低于传统调参方案。

Abstract: Forecasting a stochastic dynamical system rarely means a single number: one wants several observables---future state, threshold event, regime label---each with its own likelihood. Standard multi-task recipes balance per-task losses, tuned or learned. We instead compose the observables' likelihoods in per-task free-routed last-layer beliefs on a shared backbone; this absorbs unit-dependent loss scaling into likelihood parameters learned in the same gradient pass. Stochastic dynamics supply what static benchmarks cannot: computable ground truth for the predictive variance. Results land where theory puts them: on the well-specified, homoscedastic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process the learned predictive law recovers the analytic kernel and correctly specified baselines tie. On heteroscedastic systems (stochastic Lorenz-63, real air-quality data) the belief's input-dependent variance separates: best single-run NLL on the state and regime tasks, calibration matched only by arms whose NLL it beats, at a fraction of the tuned grids' cost. On the real series the state margin holds across five rolling origins.

</details>


### [186] [Quantum-Inspired Modeling of Driving Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25907)
*Mohammad Elayan,Omid Armantalab,Wissam Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子启发的驾驶员行为表示方法，将每位驾驶员编码为一个随时间演化的密度矩阵，统一表征行为的不确定性、时间演化与情境依赖的变化。在I-24 MOTION数据集上无监督训练，恢复出自由流、过渡、拥堵三种可解释的驾驶模式，并能复现宏观交通现象（基本图与滞后回线），还可为经典跟驰模型提供情境依赖参数，并为自动驾驶车辆提供周围驾驶员的行为实时读取与短期运动预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶员行为具有异质性、情境依赖性和时变性，这些特性共同塑造了可观察的交通现象。然而大多数模型预先固定了行为变量之间的交互方式和形式，超出该形式的行为被当作噪声吸收；而足够灵活的模型又往往丧失可解释性。作者希望构建一种既连续、概率化、情境依赖、历史依赖，又能从数据中学习行为变量间交互的统一表示，同时保持可解释性。

Method: 借鉴量子力学的数学框架，每位驾驶员被表示为一个演化的密度矩阵（density matrix）。该表示同时具备连续性、概率性、情境依赖性和历史依赖性，且行为变量间的交互关系由数据驱动学习。训练采用无监督方式，使用I-24 MOTION数据集。训练后从表示中恢复出三个可解释的驾驶模式（自由流、过渡、拥堵），并将该表示为经典跟驰模型供给上下文相关参数，同时用于对周围驾驶员的实时行为读取和短期运动预测。

Result: 框架无监督地从I-24 MOTION数据集中恢复了三种可解释的驾驶模式，对应自由流、过渡和拥堵三种状态；这些模式准确捕捉了数据的行为范围以及驾驶员在状态间随条件变化的平滑过渡；该表示还能复现宏观现象，与基本图一致并再现滞后回线；此外它能为经典跟驰模型提供情境依赖参数，并赋予自动驾驶车辆对周边驾驶员的实时行为读取与短时运动预测能力。

Conclusion: 该量子启发的驾驶员行为表示框架为交通建模提供了一种兼具可解释性与可信性的新路径，统一了此前通常被分开或部分处理的行为属性，并支持从微观个体行为到宏观交通现象的跨尺度建模。团队发布了开源工具包以支持数据预处理、训练、推理与分析。

Abstract: Driver behavior is heterogeneous, context-dependent, and changes over time, and these properties shape the traffic phenomena we observe. Most models, however, fix in advance which behavioral variables interact and how. Behavior outside that form is absorbed as noise, while models flexible enough to capture it tend to lose interpretability. We introduce a quantum-inspired representation of driver behavior that combines properties usually treated separately or in part: it is continuous, probabilistic, context-dependent, history-dependent, and represents interactions among behavioral variables as learned from data. Each driver is encoded as an evolving density matrix, providing a unified representation of behavioral uncertainty, temporal evolution, and context-dependent behavioral variation. Trained without supervision on the I-24 MOTION dataset, the framework recovers three interpretable driving profiles representing three regimes: free flow, transition, and congestion. The profiles capture the behavioral range of the data and the smooth transitions drivers make between regimes as conditions change. The same representation also reproduces known macroscopic phenomena, aligning with the fundamental diagram and reproducing hysteresis loops. We also show how the representation supports practical use: it supplies context-dependent parameters to classical car-following models, and gives an autonomous vehicle a live behavioral read of the surrounding drivers with a short-horizon forecast of their motion. The framework points toward models of traffic that are interpretable and trustworthy by construction. We release an open-source toolkit on GitHub (https://github.com/mselayan/quantum-driver-representation) spanning data processing, training, inference, and analysis.

</details>


### [187] [One Symptom, Three Levers: A Critical Review of On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25936)
*Justin Robert,Raheel Qader*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇综述论文聚焦于On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）方法——一种让语言模型以自身为教师进行在线蒸馏的训练范式。作者指出该领域当前的主导失败模式是"塌缩"（collapse），即模型生成推理路径集合的逐渐狭窄化。论文提出塌缩受三个杠杆控制：信号应用位置（token加权方式）、教师所见内容（特权信息性质）、以及信号变化时机（教师动态与指导衰减）。综述将范围限定在数学推理领域，不报告新实验，贡献在于建立统一术语框架并厘清已有结论与争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD作为一种去除额外教师模型成本的蒸馏方法，虽然早期结果显示出与强化学习相当的精度且只需少量生成token，但其核心的不对称性——教师不比学生强只比学生信息多——导致了信号偏差，进而引发"塌缩"这一主导性失败模式。由于不同论文对塌缩现象命名各异、缺乏统一框架，作者希望提供结构化视角来整合该领域的发现。

Method: 本文是综述性论文，不报告新实验。作者提出将塌缩视为受三个杠杆控制的症状：（1）信号应用位置——token级别的加权方式；（2）教师所见内容——特权信息的性质（如参考答案、计划、环境反馈）；（3）信号变化时机——教师动态与指导随训练进程的衰减策略。论文将范围限定在数学推理领域，因为该方法是该领域起源且失败模式记录最为完善。

Result: 论文提供了一个结构化的分析框架，用三个杠杆统一描述塌缩的成因与调控方式。同时建立了一套共享词汇表，映射不同论文中对同一现象的多种命名，并明确区分了该领域中哪些结论已被确证、哪些仍存在争议。

Conclusion: 塌缩并非OPSD特有，但特权信息会加剧该问题。本文的核心贡献不在新实验而在结构性梳理：统一的术语体系、三杠杆分析框架，以及对于已定论与待争论之事的清晰划分，为该领域的后续研究提供了共同语言和方向指引。

Abstract: On-policy distillation trains a language model on its own generations while a teacher scores them token by token. It combines the dense supervision of imitation learning with the on-policy sampling of reinforcement learning. But it requires a second, larger model to act as teacher. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) removes that cost. The teacher is the model itself, conditioned on privileged information the student will not have at test time, such as a reference solution, a plan, or environment feedback. The teacher is no stronger than the student, only better informed. Early results were promising, with accuracy comparable to reinforcement learning at a fraction of the generated tokens. But the same asymmetry that produces the signal also biases it. One failure mode now dominates the field: collapse, the progressive narrowing of the set of reasoning paths the model can produce. Collapse is not specific to OPSD, though privileged information aggravates it. This review treats collapse as a symptom governed by three levers: (i) where the signal is applied, that is, how tokens are weighted; (ii) what the teacher is shown, that is, the nature of the privileged information; and (iii) when the signal changes, that is, the teacher's dynamics and the decay of guidance. We restrict our scope to mathematical reasoning, where the method originated and where its failure modes are best documented. We report no new experiments. The contribution is structural: a shared vocabulary for phenomena named differently across papers, and a clear line between what is settled and what is still disputed.

</details>


### [188] [When Pruning Meets Interpretability: Preserving Sparse Autoencoder Robustness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25941)
*Suchit Gupte,Xueru Zhang,Mohammad Mahdi Khalili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了后置模型剪枝对稀疏自编码器（SAE）行为的影响。理论上证明剪枝对固定SAE的影响由扰动能量（一种协方差加权范数）决定，揭示了幅度剪枝忽略激活几何结构从而破坏SAE功能的问题，而Wanda和SparseGPT等激活感知方法能隐式控制扰动能量因此更稳健。研究还发现所有剪枝方法对中间层更敏感，据此提出分层稀疏度分配策略，在四个模型架构上验证了理论发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE被广泛用于解释LLM内部表示，但其在模型压缩（剪枝）后的可靠性尚未被系统理解。现有研究缺乏对剪枝如何影响SAE行为的理论分析和实证评估。

Method: 1) 从理论上刻画剪枝对SAE的影响，将其归结为扰动能量（协方差加权范数）；2) 系统比较幅度剪枝与激活感知方法（Wanda、SparseGPT）对SAE行为的影响；3) 揭示中间层对剪枝更敏感的结构性脆弱性；4) 提出基于层级敏感度的分层稀疏度分配策略。

Result: 幅度剪枝因忽略激活几何结构会扭曲表示空间并破坏SAE功能；激活感知方法能更稳健地保持SAE行为；所有方法中间层剪枝敏感性更高；提出的分层稀疏度分配策略在相同平均剪枝稀疏度下实现更低的困惑度；四个模型架构上的实验验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 剪枝对SAE的影响本质上由扰动能量决定，选择考虑激活几何结构的剪枝方法并针对层的敏感性进行差异化稀疏度分配，可以显著提升压缩后模型的可解释性稳健性。代码已公开。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret the internal representations of large language models (LLMs), yet their reliability under post-hoc model compression remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of how pruning affects SAE behavior and theoretically show that, for a fixed SAE, its impact is governed by perturbation energy, a covariance-weighted norm. This perspective exposes a key limitation of magnitude pruning: by ignoring activation geometry, it distorts the learned representation space and degrades SAE functionality. Activation-aware methods such as Wanda and SparseGPT, in contrast, implicitly control perturbation energy and are therefore substantially more robust at preserving SAE behavior. We further reveal a consistent structural vulnerability across all pruning methods: middle layers are significantly more sensitive to pruning than early or late layers. Guided by this insight, we propose a layer-wise sparsity allocation strategy, achieving lower perplexity under the same average pruning sparsity. Experiments across four model architectures validate our theoretical findings. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/osu-srml/sae-robustness-under-pruning/tree/main.

</details>


### [189] [Spectral Allocation: Why Muon Outperforms Adam, and How to Improve Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25990)
*Xiaodong Wu,Wenyi Yu,Chao Zhang,Philip Woodland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过试验外（out-of-sample）频谱探针分析揭示了正交优化器（如Muon）在Transformer预训练中优于Adam的机理：损失景观的频谱剖面呈各向异性——"头部"方向在高失稳边缘运行需小步长，而"本体"方向可容忍大步长。基于此发现，作者提出频谱感知优化器SAMuon及其简化版SAMuon-lite，通过对本体系数放大来弥补Muon均匀缩放对本体利用不足的缺陷，在124M到1B参数的modded-nanogpt模型上以更少训练token（节省13.3%-24.0%）达到同等验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Muon等正交优化器相对Adam能显著加速大语言模型预训练，但其内在机理仍未被完全理解。作者希望通过频谱分析框架，从损失景观的角度解释不同优化器的性能差异（Muon优于Adam优于SGD），并据此识别Muon的不足，从而设计更优的优化器。

Method: 作者在真实训练轨迹的检查点上，对每个动量缓冲进行奇异值分解，并在留出数据上沿每个奇异方向估计损失最优步长，得到频谱剖面。基于该剖面揭示MUon均匀缩放的局限（本体仍被利用不足），提出SAMuon：保持头部（head）处于Muon尺度，用静态频谱先验放大本体（bulk）。提供两个变体：完整版使用低秩随机化SVD，精简版SAMuon-lite使用秩一幂迭代的两级近似。理论部分证明精确白化版本的两种变体在标准假设下保持Muon的渐进收敛率。

Result: 实验表明频谱剖面在批次和训练阶段间稳定，且跨优化器和模型规模一致。SAMuon在124M-1B参数的modded-nanogpt模型上，所有评估的模型规模和批大小配置中均优于调优的AdamW和Muon（Scion实现）基线，比Muon减少13.3%-24.0%的训练token以达到相同验证损失；SAMuon-lite保留了大部分增益且几乎无壁钟开销。

Conclusion: 论文首次从频谱分配视角统一解释了正交优化器加速Transformer预训练的机理，并据此设计了超越Muon的SAMuon优化器。该工作不仅提供了可操作的性能提升方案，也为优化器设计提供了"频谱感知"这一新原则，表明针对损失景观的频谱结构自适应调节各方向步长是进一步提升预训练效率的关键方向。

Abstract: Orthogonal optimisers such as Muon can substantially accelerate large language model pretraining relative to Adam, yet the mechanism remains incompletely understood. We investigate this through an out-of-sample spectral probing analysis of Transformer loss landscapes. At checkpoints along real training trajectories, we decompose each momentum buffer into its singular directions and estimate the loss-optimal step size along each direction on held-out data. The resulting spectral profile is anisotropic yet stable across batches and training stages, and consistent across the optimisers and model scales: a volatile head operating at the Edge-of-Stability supports a much smaller step size than the tolerant bulk, which permits substantially larger steps. This profile provides a unified spectral allocation account of why Muon outperforms Adam, which outperforms SGD. It also exposes a limitation of Muon's uniform scaling: it still underutilises the bulk. Guided by this finding, we introduce Spectral-Aware Muon (SAMuon), which holds the head at the Muon scale and amplifies the bulk using a static spectral prior. We provide two variants: the complete SAMuon follows the measured profile using a low-rank randomised SVD and the simplified SAMuon-lite uses a two-level approximation via rank-one power iteration. Neither method adds persistent optimiser state or notable extra FLOPs beyond Muon at scale, and the idealised exact-whitening versions of both retain Muon's asymptotic convergence rate under standard assumptions. Across "modded-nanogpt" models from 124M to 1B parameters, both variants outperform tuned AdamW and Muon (Scion implementation) baselines in all evaluated model-scale and batch-size configurations. SAMuon requires 13.3% to 24.0% fewer training tokens to reach the same validation loss as Muon, while SAMuon-lite retains most of this gain with near-zero wall-clock overhead.

</details>


### [190] [DualOPSD: Adaptive Privileged Teachers for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26019)
*Yutong Chen,Guangfu Guo,Zhichao Xu,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualOPSD，一种非对称交替式自蒸馏框架，通过让特权教师沿学生分布轨迹更新以适应学习者的演化，在多个数学基准上显著优于固定教师的OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD使用一个固定的特权教师副本提供密集监督，但训练过程中学生分布和输出风格会发生变化，固定的教师无法持续提供有效监督，导致监督信号与学生分布失配。

Method: DualOPSD采用非对称交替更新框架：学生先从特权教师学习，随后教师沿同一学生轨迹向更新的学生分布移动，使后续监督对学习者更敏感，且无需额外rollout。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B非思考模式下，DualOPSD在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上avg@12分别较OPSD提升23.61、13.89和10.00分；1.7B和4B规模实验表明精度增益依赖模型规模；三个规模下均减少截断，4B诊断显示师生双向KL均降低。

Conclusion: DualOPSD通过交替更新教师以适配学生分布变化，有效提升了自蒸馏的监督质量，在数学推理任务上取得一致改进，且效果随规模增大而增强。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses a privileged copy of the student model to provide dense supervision without an external teacher. OPSD keeps this privileged teacher fixed, even though the student distribution and output style change during training. We propose DualOPSD, an asymmetric alternating framework that adapts both policies. The student first learns from the privileged teacher. The teacher then moves toward the updated student distribution on the same student trajectory. This update makes later supervision responsive to the learner and does not require another rollout. On Qwen3-8B in non-thinking mode, DualOPSD improves avg@12 over OPSD by 23.61, 13.89, and 10.00 points on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025. Results at 1.7B and 4B show that the accuracy gain depends on model scale. Across all three scales, DualOPSD reduces truncation. The 4B diagnostic also shows lower KL in both directions between the teacher and student.

</details>


### [191] [Robust CurveMoE: Multi-Norm Adversarial Defense for Mixture-of-Experts Models via Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26043)
*Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Robust CurveMoE，一种高效的混合专家（MoE）框架，通过低损耗路径连接针对不同扰动范数特化的模型，利用模式间的互补鲁棒性，在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上显著提升了干净准确率、范数特化准确率和Union准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多范数对抗防御方法通常在一个单一参数配置下优化相互竞争的鲁棒性目标，导致训练成本高昂且存在不利的鲁棒性权衡。

Method: 1) 从鲁棒性约束的曲线位置推导出干净和范数特化的专家，并仅对关键层进行选择性专家化，其余参数在路由路径间共享。2) 提出贡献引导的部分更新策略，利用基于初始化的梯度分数选择有影响力的曲线参数，以降低曲线构建成本。3) 从理论上界定了部分优化与完整曲线优化之间的目标差距。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上与WideResNet和Vision Transformer架构的实验表明，Robust CurveMoE在干净准确率、范数特化准确率和Union准确率上均优于MSD和ERMC。其中，在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上分别比最强基线提高了2.37和2.13个百分点的Union准确率。

Conclusion: Robust CurveMoE通过低损耗曲线路径连接范数特化模型，结合选择性专家化和贡献引导的部分更新，有效缓解了多范数对抗防御中的鲁棒性权衡问题，并大幅降低了训练开销。

Abstract: Multi-norm adversarial defense aims to protect neural networks against perturbations defined by different norm constraints, but existing methods typically optimize competing robustness objectives within a single parameter configuration, leading to substantial training cost and unfavorable robustness trade-offs. We propose Robust CurveMoE, an efficient mixture-of-experts framework that connects models specialized for different perturbation norms through a low-loss path and exploits the complementary robustness profiles of models along this path. Robust CurveMoE derives clean and norm-specialized experts from robustness-constrained curve locations and selectively expertizes only influential layers, while sharing the remaining parameters across routing paths. To further reduce curve-construction cost, we introduce contribution-guided partial updating, which selects influential curve parameters using initialization-based gradient scores. We also theoretically bound the objective gap between partial and full curve optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100 with WideResNet and Vision Transformer architectures show that Robust CurveMoE consistently improves clean, norm-specific, and Union accuracy over MSD and ERMC. In particular, it improves Union accuracy by 2.37 and 2.13 percentage points over the strongest baseline on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100, respectively. Extensive ablations further validate the effectiveness of partial updating, selective expertization, and robustness-constrained expert selection.

</details>


### [192] [How Much Rank Does LoRA Need? Rank-Error Bounds for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26052)
*Gerard Conangla Planes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Transformer注意力机制中的LoRA（低秩适配）更新建立了任务相关的近似误差理论。通过固定预训练注意力头、目标注意力函数和下游任务输入分布，推导了rank-r查询LoRA更新在预期KL散度下可达到的最小误差的上下界，并揭示了softmax饱和、多注意力头融合及查询/键联合更新对所需rank的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA更新中rank的选择通常是经验性的、缺乏理论基础的任务。本文旨在为LoRA rank选择提供理论依据，特别是针对Transformer注意力机制的近似误差进行严格分析，将rank选择从经验实践提升到可量化的理论问题。

Method: 作者采用谱分析和概率论方法：首先对单查询LoRA更新建立KL误差下界（与ψ(‖d‖₂)成正比）和无条件上界（min{‖d‖₂²/4, √2‖d‖₂}）；然后在显式可实现性、几何和矩条件下，将最优rank-r误差界在ψ(√T_r)的显式倍数与min{T_r/4, √2T_r}之间，其中T_r是下游加权的目标更新尾部能量；此外还提供目标Fisher界、无约束下界以及softmax饱和结构的构造性分析，并扩展到多注意力头融合LoRA和联合查询/键更新场景。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) KL误差下界与ψ(‖d‖₂)成正比；2) 无条件上界为min{‖d‖₂²/4, √2‖d‖₂}；3) 最优rank-r误差介于ψ(√T_r)的显式倍数与min{T_r/4, √2T_r}之间；4) 在目标概率远离零和有界候选分数条件下提供Fisher界；5) 当token子集承载大部分概率质量时提供无约束下界；6) 构造了softmax饱和使匹配注意力函数所需rank小于匹配有限logits所需rank的显式家族；7) 揭示多注意力头LoRA的rank共享效应和查询/键联合更新的分解约束。

Conclusion: 该工作为LoRA rank选择提供了严格的理论指导，建立了下游任务数据分布与所需LoRA rank之间的定量关系。关键发现是软max饱和可以降低匹配注意力函数所需的rank，以及多注意力头共享rank和查询/键联合更新会引入额外的结构性约束，这些结果对实际LoRA微调中rank的超参数选择具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Choosing the rank of a low-rank adaptation (LoRA) update is usually an empirical task. In this paper, we provide a task-dependent theory of the approximation error achievable at each LoRA rank for Transformer attention. We fix a pretrained attention head, a target attention function, and a distribution over inputs from the downstream task, and bound the smallest expected Kullback--Leibler (KL) error achievable by a rank-$r$ query LoRA update. When target attention probabilities are bounded away from zero, we prove a lower bound of the error proportional to $ψ(\|d\|_2)$, where $d$ is the difference between candidate and target attention scores and $ψ(t)=\min\{t^2,t\}$. We also prove an unconditional upper bound $\min\{\|d\|_2^2/4,\sqrt2\|d\|_2\}$. Under explicit realizability, geometry, and moment conditions, we then bound the best rank-$r$ error between an explicit multiple of $ψ(\sqrt{T_r})$ and $\min\{T_r/4,\sqrt{2T_r}\}$, where $T_r$ is the downstream-weighted tail energy of the target update. We also provide target-Fisher bounds when candidate scores remain within a fixed range of the target scores, and an unrestricted lower bound when a subset of tokens carries most of the probability mass. These spectral bounds describe finite-score approximation. We then construct explicit families in which softmax saturation makes the rank required to match the attention function strictly smaller than the rank required to match the finite logits. Finally, we extend the analysis to fused multi-head LoRA and joint query/key updates, exposing the effects of rank sharing and query/key factorization constraints.

</details>


### [193] [Group-Shared Low-Rank Approximation for Mobile-Efficient Pointwise Convolutions in Large-Kernel CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26069)
*Hao Luo,Yiting Yang,Wenyi Zhao,Man Jiang,Zhijun Lin,Ghulam Mohiuddin,Ting Jiang,Kunming Luo,Zihao Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Guoqing Wang,Wei Dong,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Channel Group-Shared (CGS)低秩近似方法，通过SVD分解和通道组间共享投影矩阵的策略，大幅压缩大核CNN（如RepLKNet、ConvNeXt、SLaK）中点卷积的参数量（点卷积占模型参数>87%），在保持竞争性能的同时显著降低存储成本，使大规模预训练大核CNN模型能够在资源受限的边缘设备（如4-12GB RAM智能手机）上可行部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大核CNN虽然通过扩大感受野在视觉任务中表现出色，但其点卷积（pointwise convolutions）参数量占模型总参数量的87%以上，成为边缘设备部署的主要瓶颈。现有高效架构（如深度可分离卷积）主要关注压缩深度卷积，忽视了点卷积这一关键参数瓶颈，导致预训练大核CNN模型因存储和内存加载限制而无法在资源受限的边缘设备上部署。

Method: 提出CGS低秩近似策略：基于SVD分解构造与SVD同构的结构化低秩范式，在层内的不同通道组之间共享高参数成本的降维/升维投影矩阵，同时保留各通道组特有的低参数成本可缩放对角矩阵。这种组共享设计在保持SVD分解有效性的同时大幅减少参数量。

Result: 实验表明，经过CGS增强的大核CNN（RepLKNet、ConvNeXt、SLaK）在保持竞争性能的同时显著降低了存储成本。CGS通过缓解存储约束、降低加载时的内存带宽压力和最小化模型加载延迟，成功实现了预训练大核CNN模型在边缘设备上的可行部署。

Conclusion: CGS有效地弥合了高性能视觉模型与边缘部署之间的鸿沟，通过针对点卷积这一主要参数瓶颈的创新的、同构于SVD的组共享低秩策略，实现了大核CNN在资源受限设备上的实用部署，在性能与存储效率之间取得了有利平衡。

Abstract: Large-kernel Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) deliver remarkable performance in vision tasks by significantly expanding receptive fields, yet their quadratic parameter growth critically impedes storage-efficient edge deployment. While existing efficient architectures adopt parameter-efficient depthwise separable convolution backbones that leverage techniques like low-rank approximation and weight sharing to compress depthwise convolutions, we identify a critical oversight: pointwise convolutions dominate parameter volume (>87% in models like RepLKNet-31B) and constitute the primary deployment bottleneck on resource-constrained edge devices. This results in prohibitive storage costs and severe memory-loading constraints on resource-limited devices (e.g., smartphones with 4-12 GB Random Access Memory (RAM)). To overcome this, we propose Channel Group-Shared (CGS) low-rank approximation, a novel Singular Value Decomposition (SVD)-based parameter-sharing strategy. CGS constructs a structured low-rank paradigm isomorphic to SVD decomposition, comprising shared (high-parameter-cost) down/up-projection matrices across channel groups within a layer and channel-group-specific (low-parameter-cost) scalable diagonal matrices. This group-sharing design achieves significant parameter reduction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that large-kernel CNNs (RepLKNet, ConvNeXt, SLaK) enhanced with CGS strike an empirically favorable balance between competitive performance and substantially reduced storage costs. Crucially, by alleviating storage constraints, reducing memory bandwidth pressure during loading, and minimizing model loading latency, CGS enables the feasible deployment of pre-trained large-kernel CNN models on edge devices, thereby bridging the gap between high-performance vision models and practical edge deployment.

</details>


### [194] [ICON Decomposition: Multivariate Concept-Level Explanations of Deep Representations for Model Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26083)
*Roshan Prakash Rane,Marco Simnacher,Manuel Pfeuffer,Marc-Andre Schulz,Nys Tjade Siegel,Maximilian Dreyer,Frederik Pahde,Wojciech Samek,Sonja Greven,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ICON分解方法，通过方差分解量化每个概念在控制其他概念和结果变量后对网络层方差的解释比例，从而更准确地识别深度神经网络中的捷径学习（虚假关联）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念可解释性方法通常孤立地评估每个概念是否可从网络层解码，忽略了概念之间的相关性，导致将概念间的相关关系误判为模型使用这些概念的证据。需要一种能够考虑所有概念并分解出真实贡献的方法。

Method: ICON分解方法量化每个概念在控制所有其他概念和结果变量（outcome）之后，对网络层方差的解释量。通过在合成数据上与七个基线方法对比验证其有效性，并在皮肤病变和脑成像模型上应用，通过重训练和分布外测试验证解释结果。

Result: 在已知ground truth的合成数据上，ICON比七种替代基线方法更准确地恢复概念重要性。在实际应用（皮肤病变和脑成像模型）中，能隔离模型真正依赖的概念，量化未被给定概念解释的表征部分，并产生稀疏的解释，且通过重训练和分布外测试得到验证。

Conclusion: ICON分解能够更准确地识别深度网络中概念的真实重要性，避免由概念间相关性导致的虚假发现，为概念级可解释性提供更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often exploit spurious associations in their training data, a failure known as shortcut learning. Concept-based explainability methods screen for shortcuts by testing whether concepts such as a patient's sex or scanner settings can be decoded from a network layer. Because each concept is evaluated in isolation, these methods can mistake correlations between concepts as evidence that the model uses them. We introduce ICON decomposition, which instead quantifies how much of a layer's variance each concept explains after accounting for all other concepts and the outcome. On synthetic data with known ground truth, ICON recovers concept importance more accurately than seven alternative baseline methods. On skin-lesion and brain-imaging models, it isolates the concepts on which a model genuinely relies, quantifies the representation unexplained by any of the supplied concepts, and yields sparse explanations that we validate by retraining and out-of-distribution testing.

</details>


### [195] [TraceML: An Empirical Analysis of Human-Agent Planning in Machine Learning Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26086)
*Jiarui Yan,Weiwei Sun,Sijie Li,Wenhan Li,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍TraceML，一个版本级轨迹数据集，包含4,465条人类Kaggle轨迹与207条Agent轨迹，将LLM智能体在机器学习开发上的失败归因于其行为模式（狭窄循环、缺乏转轴与回退）而非能力不足，并验证了从人类实践中提炼的规划提示可部分弥合该差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于结果的基准只能记录LLM智能体与人类在端到端ML开发上的差距，却无法解释其成因，因为最终评分掩盖了开发过程中的行为模式差异。

Method: 构建TraceML：在同一比赛上配对收集人类Kaggle轨迹与两类Agent行为（Codex与MLEvolve）的版本级数据（含分数、时间戳、动作标签、意图、编辑规模及分数影响），随后从人类轨迹中蒸馏出规划提示并应用于Agent。

Result: 专家型人类会交替进行数据、验证、模型与集成工作并回退到已搁置方案；而Codex沉浸于集成权重与提交调优，MLEvolve仅原地修改模型。加入从人类实践提炼的规划提示后，Agent在指明行为上向人类靠拢且分数提升，但整体努力分布仍呈Agent形态——指令只能弥合可归约于指令的那部分差距。

Conclusion: TraceML说明LLM智能体在自主ML开发中的短板本质是其行为策略的贫乏（开发轨迹定型为狭窄循环），而非简单的编码能力不足；指示性提示仅能部分修复该差距，剩余部分源于Agent在探索与回退等元行为上的结构性不足。

Abstract: Large language models write correct code for isolated problems but remain far weaker at autonomous machine-learning development, where an agent must revise data pipelines, models, and validation over hours of feedback, and on most competitions still finishes below strong human competitors. Outcome-based benchmarks record this gap but not its cause, because they grade the final submission and discard the development process behind it. We introduce TraceML, which pairs human and agent work on the same competitions under one version-level schema: 4,465 human Kaggle trajectories across 134 competitions, seven of which are also worked by two agent scaffolds, giving 430 paired human and 207 agent trajectories. Every code version carries its score, its timestamp, and labels for the action taken, its intent, the edit size, and the score effect. Read this way, the gap becomes concrete. Experts alternate data work, validation, model changes, and ensembling, and return to approaches they had set aside. Each agent scaffold instead collapses into a narrow loop: Codex spends its steps re-weighting ensembles and tuning submissions, MLEvolve mutates its model in place, and neither pivots at the human rate nor reopens abandoned work. A short planning prompt distilled from human practice moves the behaviors it names toward the human profile and lifts scores, but the effort profile stays agent-shaped: instruction closes only the part of the gap that reduces to instructions. We release the corpus, the schema, the labelers, and the extraction pipeline at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jerryyan/TraceML.

</details>


### [196] [Agentic Autoresearch for Cell-Edge Power Control: Radically Redefining the Researcher's Role](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26093)
*Ahmad Khan,Akram Bin Sediq,Sara Azadegi Naeini,Raviraj S. Adve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将无线资源管理算法的设计层（架构、损失函数、训练配方）完全交由自主AI智能体完成。采用autoresearch协议，AI编码智能体编辑训练脚本、运行固定预算实验，并根据单一固定指标决定保留或丢弃修改。在总和最小百分位速率功率控制任务上，智能体在81次无人值守实验/26小时内达到收敛MM参考99.5%的性能，推理成本降低约600倍，且发现的输出参数化在最小百分位处精确复现最大-最小最优分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上机器学习无线资源管理算法的设计（架构、损失函数、训练配方）需要人工逐一指定，劳动强度大。作者希望证明这一设计层可以完全交由自主智能体完成，从而消除人工设计负担。

Method: 采用autoresearch协议：AI编码智能体编辑训练脚本，运行固定预算实验，依据单一不可变指标决定是否保留改动。智能体拥有对架构族、输入表示、输出参数化、损失函数和任务采样规律的完全控制权。任务为多小区网络中sum-least-percentile-rate功率控制（非凸、非光滑、强NP难）。配备安全措施：哈希固定评估器、强制推理契约、每实验预注册验证器。

Result: 在81次无人值守实验、26小时运行中，智能体在单次固定成本推理中达到收敛的minorization-maximization参考性能的99.5%，推理成本降低约600倍，从其首个可行架构起弥合了94%的性能差距，且一组参数适用于所有网络规模和百分位目标。发现的输出参数化在最小百分位处精确复现最大-最小最优分配，证明其恢复的是可证明的结构而非调优常数。

Conclusion: AI智能体可以完全自主完成无线资源管理算法的设计层工作，在极具挑战性的NP-难问题上达到接近收敛参考的性能，同时显著降低推理成本。其发现的解具有可解释的可证明结构，而非经验调参结果，表明自主设计不仅能匹配人工设计，还能揭示潜在的理论规律。

Abstract: Designing machine learning algorithms for wireless resource management is labour-intensive: the architecture, the loss function and the training recipe are all specified by hand. We demonstrate that this design layer can be surrendered to an autonomous agent in its entirety. We adopt the autoresearch protocol, in which an AI coding agent edits a training script, runs a fixed-budget experiment, and retains or discards the change according to a single immutable metric. We grant the agent authority over the architecture family, the input representation, the output parameterization, the loss function and the task-sampling law, and set it a target chosen for its difficulty: sum-least-percentile-rate power control across a multicell network. The formulation targets cell-edge throughput and is non-convex, non-smooth and strongly NP-hard away from its max-min vertex. Safeguards render the results trustworthy: a hash-pinned evaluator, an enforced inference contract and a pre-registered falsifier per experiment. In eighty-one unattended experiments over twenty-six hours, the agent reached $99.5\%$ of a converged minorization-maximization reference in one fixed-cost inference pass, at roughly $600\times$ lower inference cost, closing $94\%$ of the gap from its first working architecture, with one parameter set serving every network size and percentile target. It recovered provable structure rather than tuned constants: the output parameterization it discovered reproduces the exact max-min-optimal allocation at the minimum percentile, for every value of the trained weights.

</details>
